proposed_invariants:
- Every query that reads varieties or their referencing records states explicitly
  whether archived varieties are included, with no implicit default filter.
- No period figure, sale view, account or export query filters on the archived flag.
- Deleting a variety is a single guarded statement that checks the absence of history
  in the same statement, never a read followed by a delete.
- The new archive column name and its migration follow §11 and pass tests/migrations/test_naming.py,
  with a tested downgrade.
- A migration that drops or adds a column on a table referenced by foreign keys is
  tested upgrade and downgrade over rows in every referencing table, on both drivers.
- A translated IntegrityError becomes a domain refusal only after a re-read confirms
  the cause, and any other cause is re-raised.
- The archive use case and route never refuse because the variety has or lacks history;
  only the delete is guarded by history.

subtasks:
  a1-flag-and-readers:
    id: a1-flag-and-readers
    title: The archived flag, its migration, named readers replacing all(), and ADR-0031
    brief: "Add the stored fact and make every reader of varieties choose explicitly.\
      \ No route can archive yet; that is A2.\n\n**Migration `0012`** (revises 0011),\
      \ `app/migrations/versions/`:\n- **Upgrade:** a native `op.add_column('variety',\
      \ archived_at UtcDateTime nullable)`. No server default, no backfill, no batch\
      \ rebuild.\n- **Downgrade:** first check `SELECT 1 FROM variety WHERE archived_at\
      \ IS NOT NULL`. If any row matches, raise with a fixed message naming no data.\
      \ Otherwise `op.execute(\"ALTER TABLE variety DROP COLUMN archived_at\")` (native,\
      \ no rebuild).\n- **Test:** `tests/migrations/test_0012_archived_variety.py`,\
      \ parameterised over a `sqlite:///` file and a `sqlite+libsql:///` file.\n \
      \ 1. Upgrade to 0011, then seed two businesses with a row in each of `price_entry`,\
      \ `sale_line`, `stock_entry`, `stock_threshold` and `catalog_attributes`.\n\
      \  2. Upgrade to head (through the real `upgrade_to_head`). Set one `archived_at`\
      \ and assert the downgrade refuses.\n  3. Clear it and downgrade. Assert row\
      \ counts are unchanged and `PRAGMA foreign_key_check` is empty.\n  4. Upgrade\
      \ again.\n- The naming test (§11) must pass with no allowlist change.\n\n**Table\
      \ and domain:**\n- `VarietyRow.archived_at` (`UtcDateTime | None`).\n- `Variety`\
      \ gains `archived_at: datetime | None` and an `is_archived` property.\n\n**Port\
      \ (`app/varieties/application/ports.py`) and `SqlVarieties`:**\n- Remove `all()`.\n\
      - Add `listed()` (unarchived, by `name_key`) and `archived()` (archived, by\
      \ `name_key`).\n- `get(id)` answers any variety of the business, archived or\
      \ not.\n- Replace `name_taken` with `name_holder(name, *, other_than=None) ->\
      \ NameHolder | None`, where `NameHolder` is an enum `LIVE | ARCHIVED` in the\
      \ domain or application.\n- `register_variety` and `rename_variety` raise `NameTaken`\
      \ for any holder. A2 splits `ARCHIVED` into its own code.\n\n**Error:** `VarietyArchived(LookupError)`,\
      \ code `variety.archived`, in `app/varieties/application/errors.py`.\n- Register\
      \ it in the varieties router's `TranslateErrors` under 404.\n- Add it to `web/src/api/contract.json`\
      \ with kind `gone`, and add its entry to `web/src/api/refusals.ts` (the generic\
      \ gone dispatch), so `tsc` passes.\n\n**Move every caller of `all()`:**\n- `list_varieties`\
      \ → `listed()`. `GET /varieties` keeps its shape.\n- `SqlCatalogVarieties.on`\
      \ → `listed()`.\n- `SqlStockFigures.read`'s rows → `listed()`.\n- `SqlVarietyCatalog.ids`\
      \ becomes `listed_ids()` over `listed()`.\n- `read_stock`'s `total` is summed\
      \ over every variety key in `grouped` (the unfiltered `for_varieties(None)`\
      \ load), no longer over the listed rows. Rows and total stop sharing a filter.\n\
      - Confirm that `app/varieties/adapters/import_port.py`'s `has_records` counts\
      \ the whole `variety` table, archived included. Make it an explicit count if\
      \ it went through `all()`.\n\n**Business removal (ADR-0015):** add `variety.archived_at`\
      \ to the migrated-schema classification tests' expectations. It is exported\
      \ as an ISO instant with no secret flag, which proves the export picks it up\
      \ with no change to the command.\n\n**ADR-0031**, `docs/adr/0031-*.md`, records:\n\
      - archiving as a stored nullable instant;\n- history computed on read;\n- `listed()`/`archived()`\
      \ with no default and `all()` removed;\n- the stock total summed from the unfiltered\
      \ load;\n- the native ADD and DROP COLUMN, with the downgrade refusing while\
      \ any variety is archived;\n- the delete guarded in one `DELETE … NOT (history)\
      \ RETURNING` after dependents, with the foreign key closing the insert race;\n\
      - the use case's rollback-and-reask after a refused reference;\n- the accepted\
      \ archive race (a sale landing just after an archive);\n- archive never refused\
      \ for history (PM);\n- Turso's remote protocol as untested.\n\n**Acceptance:**\n\
      - The existing suite passes unchanged in behaviour.\n- With `archived_at` set\
      \ by hand in a test, the variety is absent from `GET /varieties` and still counted\
      \ in `GET /stock`'s `total`.\n- `mypy --strict` and `ruff` are clean."
    seams:
    - Alembic upgrade/downgrade through `upgrade_to_head` over sqlite and libsql files
    - '`Variety` domain unit tests'
    - HTTP `GET /varieties` and `GET /stock` with TestClient

  a2-archive-restore:
    id: a2-archive-restore
    title: 'Archive and restore: domain read-only rule, routes, archived list, name_archived'
    brief: 'Builds on A1. Files: `app/varieties/domain/variety.py`, `application/manage_varieties.py`,
      `application/manage_prices.py`, `application/errors.py`, `adapters/router.py`,
      `adapters/schemas.py`, `adapters/sql_varieties.py`, `contract.json` and `refusals.ts`.


      **Domain:**

      - `Variety.archive(at)` sets `archived_at` only when it is `None` (idempotent).

      - `Variety.restore()` clears it (idempotent).

      - An archived `Variety` refuses `rename`, recording a price entry and deleting
      a price entry with `VarietyArchived` (`variety.archived`, 404 gone). That makes
      archived varieties read-only in the domain itself.


      **Use cases:** `archive_variety` and `restore_variety`.

      - `get` → missing answers `variety.not_found`.

      - Then call the domain method, `save` (which persists `archived_at`) and commit
      once.

      - The instant comes from the injected clock (`app/clock.py`), never from the
      request.

      - **Archive is never refused because the variety has or lacks history** (PM).

      - Restore changes nothing else: prices, entries, threshold, catalog attributes
      and the hidden flag were never touched.


      **Names:** `register_variety` and `rename_variety` raise the new `NameHeldByArchived`,
      code `variety.name_archived` (409, kind `refusal`), when `name_holder` answers
      `ARCHIVED`. It has fixed text and interpolates nothing. `LIVE` keeps `NameTaken`.

      - Add the code to `contract.json`.

      - Add its `refusals.ts` entry: field name, with text like "Ese nombre es de
      una variedad archivada. Buscala en Archivadas."


      **Routes:**

      - `POST /varieties/{id}/archive` and `POST /varieties/{id}/restore` answer 200
      with the variety.

      - `GET /varieties?archived=true` answers `archived()`.

      - `VarietyOut` (list and detail) gains `archived: bool`.

      - The router is already mounted with `upkeep_gated`, so both writes trigger
      upkeep after commit (ADR-0014). Add no new wiring.


      **Tests, HTTP first:**

      - the archive and restore columns of the removal matrix (live with and without
      history, archived, missing and another business''s);

      - archive is idempotent and restore is idempotent;

      - a renamed or price-added archived variety answers `variety.archived`;

      - an archived name in any case or spacing is refused on add and on rename with
      `variety.name_archived`;

      - `?archived=true` lists only archived varieties, by name;

      - both new routes join `tests/test_business_isolation.py` (seeder plus `BODIES`)
      and `tests/test_gated_routes.py`. Another business''s variety, archived or not,
      answers 404.'
    seams:
    - '`Variety` domain unit tests (archive, restore, read-only rule)'
    - HTTP `POST /varieties/{id}/archive`, `POST /varieties/{id}/restore`, `GET /varieties?archived=true`,
      `POST /varieties`, `PATCH /varieties/{id}`, `POST /varieties/{id}/prices`
    depends_on: []
    status: approved
    branch: agentic/GH-117.a2-archive-restore
    base: abdb47b
    reviewed_head: 7720cf76513985de8b16e13de218b1eb8bf643dd
    reason: Migration 0012 ships on this branch without the upgrade/downgrade test
      on the sqlite and libsql file drivers with rows in the five tables that reference
      variety (invariant 8); everything else in the brief checked out and its tests
      pass.
    return_category: plan-invariant
  c-readers-and-pickers:
    id: c-readers-and-pickers
    title: Stale references refused as gone in sales, stock and catalog; archived
      stock rows; per-reader hiding tests
    brief: "Builds on A1. It touches only sales', stock's and catalog's modules, never\
      \ `app/varieties/`.\n\n**Sales:** `SqlPriceBook.for_variety` (`app/sales/adapters/price_book.py`)\
      \ answers `None` for an archived variety, as for a missing one. `record_sale`\
      \ then raises `UnknownVariety`, so the answer is `sale.variety_not_found` and\
      \ Nueva venta's existing dispatch is untouched. `PlannedPriceBook` is unchanged.\n\
      \n**Stock:**\n- `VarietyCatalog` (`app/stock/application/ports.py`) gains `usable(variety_id)\
      \ -> bool` beside `exists`. `SqlVarietyCatalog` implements it from `get(id)`\
      \ and `is_archived`.\n- **Recording an entry** requires `usable`.\n- **Editing\
      \ an entry** requires `exists` when its `variety_id` is unchanged (it may keep\
      \ its own archived variety), and `usable` when the variety changes.\n- **Setting\
      \ or turning off a threshold** requires `usable`.\n- Missing still raises stock's\
      \ internal `VarietyNotFound`. Archived raises a new internal, code-less `VarietyArchived`\
      \ signal in stock's `application/errors.py`. The stock router re-raises it as\
      \ varieties' `VarietyArchived` (`variety.archived`), the same pattern it already\
      \ uses for not-found.\n- `GET /stock?archived=true` answers the archived varieties'\
      \ rows: the same `stock_level` over the same load, filtered to `archived()`.\
      \ It is used by the archived page.\n\n**Catalog:**\n- `CatalogVarieties.exists`\
      \ becomes `usable`, so editing catalog attributes of an archived variety is\
      \ refused.\n- The catalog router answers it as varieties' `VarietyArchived`\
      \ (`variety.archived`). Missing keeps `catalog.variety_not_found`.\n- The text\
      \ and the \"does not go out\" list already come from `listed()` (A1).\n\n**Tests:**\n\
      - A small test helper under `tests/fakes/` sets `archived_at` on a variety row\
      \ directly, since there is no archive route on this branch.\n- HTTP tests, each\
      \ in its owner's `tests/<capability>/`, show an archived variety:\n  - absent\
      \ from `GET /catalog` (text and \"does not go out\");\n  - absent from `GET\
      \ /panel` stock rows and `GET /stock` rows;\n  - present in `GET /stock?archived=true`.\n\
      - `GET /stock`'s `total` is unchanged by archiving.\n- `POST /sales` with an\
      \ archived variety answers 404 `sale.variety_not_found`, keeps 0 rows written,\
      \ and leaves the form-side contract unchanged.\n- `POST /stock/entries` and\
      \ the threshold route answer 404 `variety.archived`.\n- Editing an entry that\
      \ keeps its archived variety succeeds. Moving an entry onto another archived\
      \ variety answers `variety.archived`.\n- Deleting such an entry, deleting a\
      \ sale containing an archived variety, and assigning a customer to it all still\
      \ work.\n- `PUT /catalog/varieties/{id}` on an archived variety answers `variety.archived`.\n\
      - The sweep's existing assertions still pass. Add `?archived=true` on `/stock`\
      \ to its list checks."
    seams:
    - HTTP `GET /stock` and `GET /stock?archived=true`, `POST /stock/entries`, the
      entry edit and delete routes, the threshold route
    - HTTP `POST /sales`, `DELETE /sales/{id}`, assign customer
    - HTTP `GET /catalog`, `PUT /catalog/varieties/{id}`
    - HTTP `GET /panel`
    depends_on:
    - a1-flag-and-readers
    status: approved
    branch: agentic/GH-117.c-readers-and-pickers
    base: fb46393
    reviewed_head: d7a472ea96bb3cfe253a10217a7e9d1dd5f864ed
  b-delete:
    id: b-delete
    title: 'Delete a variety with no history: guarded delete, dependents, offered
      action, foreign-key race'
    brief: "Builds on A2 and C. It shares varieties' files with A2 and stock's `manage_entries`\
      \ with C.\n\n**Repository (`SqlVarieties`):**\n- One private predicate `_history_exists(variety_id)`:\
      \ two `EXISTS` subqueries over `SaleLineRow` and `StockEntryRow`, each filtered\
      \ on `variety_id` and on `business_id`. The adapter imports those tables; adapters\
      \ may cross.\n- Port methods:\n  - `has_history(variety_id) -> bool`;\n  - `remove_price_entries(variety_id)`,\
      \ a Core `delete()`;\n  - `remove_unused(variety_id) -> bool`, which runs `DELETE\
      \ FROM variety WHERE id=:id AND business_id=:b AND NOT (<history>) RETURNING\
      \ id`.\n- Use Core statements only, never mutate `row.price_entries`.\n\n**Dependents:**\
      \ varieties declares `VarietyLeaving(Protocol): def leaving(self, variety_id:\
      \ int) -> None` in `application/ports.py`.\n- Stock implements it in its adapters\
      \ by deleting that variety's `stock_threshold` row.\n- Catalog implements it\
      \ by deleting that variety's `catalog_attributes` row.\n- `app/main.py` composes\
      \ both as one `(Session, business_id)` factory on `app.state` (ADR-0006's composite).\
      \ The varieties router reads it the way other factories are read.\n\n**Use case\
      \ `delete_variety`, in order:**\n1. `get`: missing answers `variety.not_found`;\
      \ archived answers `variety.archived` (restore first).\n2. `has_history`: true\
      \ answers `VarietyHasHistory`, code `variety.has_history` (409 refusal), with\
      \ nothing written.\n3. `leaving.leaving(id)`, then `remove_price_entries`.\n\
      4. `remove_unused`: if no row comes back, `transaction.rollback()` and the same\
      \ refusal.\n5. One commit.\n\nAdd the code to `contract.json` and `refusals.ts`.\n\
      \n**Routes:**\n- `DELETE /varieties/{id}` answers 204.\n- `GET /varieties/{id}`\
      \ gains `removal: \"delete\" | \"archive\" | null`: `null` when archived, otherwise\
      \ from `has_history`. The list does not carry it.\n\n**Foreign-key race:** the\
      \ repositories that insert a reference to a variety wrap their flush. Those\
      \ are sales' record path (`sql_sales.py`), stock's entry add and save (`sql_stock_entries.py`)\
      \ and varieties' price entry save.\n- Catch `IntegrityError` only to set a flag.\n\
      - Outside the `except`, raise an internal, code-less `ReferenceRefused` (ADR-0005:\
      \ nothing of the driver's message stays reachable).\n- `record_sale`, `record_entry`,\
      \ `edit_entry` and `record_price` catch it, call `transaction.rollback()`, and\
      \ re-ask their own lookup (`price_book.for_variety`, `catalog.usable` or `exists`,\
      \ `get`).\n  - If the variety is gone or archived, raise their gone code: `sale.variety_not_found`,\
      \ `variety.not_found` or `variety.archived`.\n  - Otherwise re-raise the original\
      \ error, so another foreign-key bug stays a 500.\n\n**Tests:**\n- **Offered-action\
      \ and delete matrix over HTTP:** history from each source alone (a list line,\
      \ a gift, a special price, an entry of each origin, both adjustment signs).\
      \ Price entries, a threshold and attributes alone leave `delete`. Deleting the\
      \ last sale or entry offers `delete` again. An archived variety whose history\
      \ went away, once restored, offers `delete`.\n- A deleted variety takes its\
      \ price entries, threshold and attributes with it, and its name is accepted\
      \ again at once.\n- **Use-case tests for both re-ask branches:** a fake whose\
      \ flush raises the signal, with the lookup saying gone (gone code) and with\
      \ the lookup saying present (re-raised).\n- **Repository tests over a real SQLite\
      \ file, both `sqlite:///` and `sqlite+libsql:///`:**\n  - `remove_unused` returns\
      \ the id with no history, and nothing with history;\n  - after a forced rollback\
      \ following the dependent deletes, thresholds, attributes and price entries\
      \ are all still there;\n  - a two-connection race in both orders: delete first\
      \ means the insert is refused as gone; insert first means the delete answers\
      \ `variety.has_history`. 0 rows reference a missing variety.\n- `DELETE /varieties/{id}`\
      \ joins the isolation sweep and the gated-route test."
    seams:
    - HTTP `DELETE /varieties/{id}` and `GET /varieties/{id}`
    - use cases `delete_variety`, `record_sale`, `record_entry`, `edit_entry`, `record_price`
      with a fake repository raising `ReferenceRefused`
    - '`SqlVarieties` repository over real SQLite files (sqlite and libsql), two connections'
    depends_on:
    - a2-archive-restore
    - c-readers-and-pickers
    status: approved
    branch: agentic/GH-117.b-delete
    base: d7a472e
    conflicted_paths: []
    reviewed_head: 18a11aa8b6f53c411e211c35b2ab60924d743e61
    reason: The sale-line race has no test over real SQLite files, though the PRD
      metric names it. sql_sales.py's new flush_or_refuse is only tested with a fake
      that raises the signal, never on a real foreign-key failure, on either driver
      or in either order.
    return_category: spec-missing-test
  e-books-unchanged:
    id: e-books-unchanged
    title: 'The books don''t move: before/after-archive/after-restore capture, comes-back-whole,
      and upkeep on archive and restore'
    brief: 'Builds on A2 and C, branched from master after both merged. Test code
      only. If a figure moves, the fix lands here with the test that found it.


      **`tests/varieties/test_archive_moves_no_figure.py`**, written first, at the
      HTTP seam with the `client` fixture and the movable clock.


      Seed, through the API:

      - two varieties, one to archive;

      - a purchase;

      - sales left owing and paid on the spot, a gift and a special price;

      - a later payment;

      - a negative adjustment with costed grams and one with pending grams;

      - a sale line with pending grams;

      - an expense.


      Capture three times (before archiving, after `POST /varieties/{id}/archive`,
      after `POST /varieties/{id}/restore`) and assert 0 differences:

      - `GET /analysis?month=` for every month of the history, with its weeks, months,
      weekdays and all-time window;

      - `GET /panel`''s period figures (today, week, month);

      - every `GET /sales/{id}`, lines and costs included;

      - every customer''s balance and account;

      - `GET /stock`''s `total`;

      - the archived variety''s stock, status and pending grams. Read these from `GET
      /stock` before archiving and after restoring, and from `GET /stock?archived=true`
      while archived.


      **Comes back whole:** after restoring, the variety''s prices, entries, stock,
      threshold, catalog attributes and hidden flag equal what they were before. Run
      it once with the variety hidden and once shown, and confirm it is back in `GET
      /varieties`, `GET /catalog` and the panel''s stock rows.


      **Upkeep:** using the existing fake Telegram with a published pin, archive and
      restore each attempt an edit exactly when the built text differs from the last
      published text. A hidden or unpriced variety''s archive sends nothing. The triggering
      request answers normally even when the fake fails.'
    seams:
    - HTTP `POST /varieties/{id}/archive` and `/restore`, `GET /analysis`, `GET /panel`,
      `GET /sales/{id}`, customer balance and account routes, `GET /stock`, `GET /catalog`
    - the existing fake Telegram adapter
    depends_on:
    - a2-archive-restore
    - c-readers-and-pickers
    status: approved
    branch: agentic/GH-117.e-books-unchanged
    base: d7a472e
    conflicted_paths: []
    reviewed_head: 82d24f6948f907978e59252d9f48557c61269ad8
  d1-removal-action:
    id: d1-removal-action
    title: 'Front: the removal action on a variety''s page, its confirmations, refusals
      and two-pane reselection'
    brief: "Builds on B and E. Work in `web/src/` over `GET /varieties/{id}`'s `archived`\
      \ and `removal`, using the api layer and its types, `VariedadDetalle`, the confirmation\
      \ copy and the Variedades two-pane container.\n\n**API types:** add `archived:\
      \ boolean` and `removal: 'delete' | 'archive' | null` to the variety type. Add\
      \ client calls for archive, restore and delete.\n\n**On a live variety's page,\
      \ exactly one removal action, from `removal`:**\n- **`delete`:** \"Eliminar\
      \ variedad\" opens a destructive ConfirmDialog. Then the toast \"Variedad eliminada\"\
      \ and back to Variedades.\n- **`archive`:** \"Archivar variedad\" opens a confirmation.\n\
      \  - With stock not zero, it names the grams on hand from the stock row, formatted\
      \ `12 g`: \"Te quedan 12 g. Si la archivás, dejan de verse en Inicio y en las\
      \ listas, pero el stock queda guardado.\"\n  - With zero stock it simply asks.\
      \ The exact wording follows the design.\n  - Then the toast \"Variedad archivada\"\
      \ and back to Variedades.\n  - Toasts carry no name or amount.\n- **`variety.has_history`\
      \ on delete (stale page):** refetch the page, which then shows \"Archivar variedad\"\
      . No toast.\n- **Archive never meets a history refusal.**\n- **A gone answer**\
      \ (`variety.not_found` or `variety.archived`) on confirm closes the dialog,\
      \ refreshes the list and shows \"Eso ya no está. La lista ya está al día.\"\n\
      \n**Name refusal:** `variety.name_archived` shows inline on the name field in\
      \ the add and rename forms, keeping what was typed.\n\n**Stock entry edit form:**\
      \ editing an entry whose variety is archived shows that variety, by the entry's\
      \ own name, as the chosen one. The picker otherwise offers only `GET /varieties`\
      \ (listed) varieties.\n\n**Computer:** archiving or deleting the selected variety\
      \ selects the next one by name, else the previous one, else the empty state.\
      \ First selection needs no change, since the list is unarchived only.\n\n**Tests**\
      \ (Testing Library, `fetch` faked):\n- the action shown for each `removal` value;\n\
      - both confirmations, the grams wording and zero stock;\n- the toasts;\n- the\
      \ stale-delete refresh;\n- the gone notice;\n- `name_archived` inline;\n- the\
      \ computer reselection;\n- no name or amount in toasts, the console or storage.\n\
      \nRun the design comparison of the variety page (phone and computer, both themes)\
      \ and attach it."
    seams:
    - '`VariedadDetalle` and the Variedades two-pane screen through Testing Library
      with `fetch` faked'
    - the variety add and rename forms and the stock entry edit form through Testing
      Library
    depends_on:
    - b-delete
    - e-books-unchanged
    status: approved
    screens:
    - VariedadDetalle
    - Variedades

  d2-archivadas:
    id: d2-archivadas
    title: 'Front: Archivadas card, the read-only archived page, Archivadas (N), routes
      and the no-curl run'
    brief: 'Builds on D1. **Entry condition:** `docs/product/design-system/pantallas/`
      holds the Archivadas card and the archived variety''s page. Do not start without
      them.


      **"Archivadas (N)"** sits at the foot of Variedades, next to "Todas las entradas".
      N comes from `GET /varieties?archived=true`. The entry is absent when there
      are none.


      **The Archivadas card** at `/varieties/archived`:

      - a card with the bar or sidebar and "‹ Variedades", without "+ Nueva venta";

      - archived varieties by name, each marked "Archivada" in words, with "Restaurar";

      - Restaurar is one tap with no confirmation, shows the toast "Variedad restaurada"
      and refreshes both lists;

      - on a computer it uses two panes and, with nothing selected, selects the first
      archived variety by name;

      - back with no history goes to Variedades.


      **The archived page** is the same `/varieties/{id}` page in read-only mode when
      `archived` is true:

      - "‹ Archivadas", and back with no history goes to Archivadas;

      - the summary line and sections are read-only, with "Archivada" in words;

      - its stock comes from `GET /stock?archived=true`;

      - it has no "+ Entrada", "Nuevo precio", price delete, threshold switch, rename,
      Editar forms or removal action;

      - its only action is "Restaurar". After restoring, the page shows the live variety
      with its `removal`.


      **Routes:**

      - add `/varieties/archived` to `web/src/routes.ts` and to `scripts/design/screens.json`
      (the card and the archived page);

      - add it to `SHARED_FRONT_PATHS` in `app/static_spa.py` and to `contract.json`''s
      `sharedFrontRoutes` in this same diff (ADR-0030). The existing pytest and front
      checks cover them. The page reuses `^/varieties/[0-9]+$`.


      **Tests** (Testing Library):

      - the count and its absence;

      - the card lists only archived varieties, with "Archivada" shown;

      - restore takes one tap from the card and from the page;

      - the archived page offers no action but Restaurar;

      - the back rules;

      - computer first selection.


      **e2e no-curl run** (`web/e2e/`): delete a fresh variety, archive one with a
      sale, find it in Archivadas, open its read-only page and restore it. Run it
      on phone and computer viewports, counting taps: the action plus its confirmation.


      Run the design comparisons for the Variedades list, the variety page, Archivadas
      and the archived page (phone and computer, both themes) and attach them.'
    seams:
    - Archivadas card and `VariedadDetalle` read-only mode through Testing Library
      with `fetch` faked
    - the e2e front run
    - pytest over `SHARED_FRONT_PATHS` and `sharedFrontRoutes` (existing contract
      checks)
    depends_on:
    - d1-removal-action
    status: approved
    screens:
    - Archivadas
    - VariedadArchivada
    - Variedades
    branch: agentic/GH-117.d2-archivadas
    base: a6c3585
    reviewed_head: 59ad57b501b54f7dda8937b989be99a4138f79b3

invariants:
- Nothing reads varieties through an implicit default. A caller uses `listed()`, `archived()`
  or `get()` and reads `is_archived` itself; there is no `all()` and no `include_archived`
  flag.
- No period figure, sale view, account, Todas las entradas or export query filters
  on `archived_at`, and `GET /stock`'s `total` is summed from the unfiltered `for_varieties(None)`
  load.
- Archive and restore never refuse because the variety has or lacks history; only
  delete is guarded by history.
- A variety is deleted only by the guarded `DELETE … AND NOT (history) RETURNING id`,
  after its dependents in the same transaction, never by a read followed by an unconditional
  delete.
- An `IntegrityError` becomes a gone code only after the use case rolls back and its
  own lookup confirms the variety is gone or archived; any other cause is re-raised.
- A refusal code is added to `contract.json` exactly once, in the piece that registers
  its class, together with its `refusals.ts` entry, and no refusal interpolates a
  name or an amount.
- Every new route takes the business from `CurrentBusinessId` and joins `tests/test_business_isolation.py`
  and `tests/test_gated_routes.py` in the diff that adds it.
- Migration 0012 uses native ADD and DROP COLUMN with no table rebuild, passes `tests/migrations/test_naming.py`
  and is tested upgrade and downgrade on both the sqlite and the libsql file drivers,
  with rows in all five tables that reference `variety`.

approval_settled:
- subtask: a2-archive-restore
  location: app/migrations/versions/0012_variety_archived_at.py:20
  claim: This branch adds migration 0012, but no test runs its upgrade and downgrade
    on the sqlite and libsql file drivers with rows in all five tables that reference
    `variety`, so invariant 8 is broken as delivered.
  version: 2
- subtask: b-delete
  location: app/shared/reference.py:9
  claim: Invariant 5 is not met. No repository turns an IntegrityError into ReferenceRefused,
    and record_sale, record_entry, edit_entry and record_price never roll back and
    re-ask their lookup, so a sale, entry or price that races a delete still comes
    back as a raw 500.
  version: 3
- subtask: b-delete
  location: tests/varieties
  claim: 'The brief requires two kinds of tests at declared seams, and neither exists:
    use-case tests where a fake raises ReferenceRefused (one with the lookup saying
    gone, one saying present), and SqlVarieties repository tests over real sqlite:///
    and sqlite+libsql:/// files (remove_unused, rollback after the dependent deletes,
    a two-connection race in both orders).'
  version: 3
- subtask: b-delete
  location: app/varieties/application/manage_varieties.py:88
  claim: Nothing tests the rollback branch of delete_variety, where remove_unused
    returns False after the dependent deletes. The brief asks for a test that thresholds,
    attributes and price entries all survive the forced rollback.
  version: 3
- subtask: b-delete
  location: tests/varieties/test_sql_varieties_delete.py:209
  claim: The two-connection race over real sqlite and libsql files covers stock entries
    and price entries but never a sale line, in either order, so sql_sales.py's new
    flush_or_refuse is never run against a real foreign-key failure.
  version: 3
- subtask: d1-removal-action
  location: web/src/screens/varieties/VarietyNew.tsx:31
  claim: The brief requires a Testing Library test that `variety.name_archived` shows
    inline on the name field of the add form and keeps what was typed, but no test
    exercises the add form with that code; only the rename form is covered.
  version: 2
- subtask: integration
  location: web/src/screens/varieties/Archivadas.test.tsx:54
  claim: 'Every Archivadas test runs as a phone (setComputer(false)). No Testing Library
    test covers the computer behaviour the brief lists under Tests: ArchivedIndex
    selecting the first archived variety by name, or VarietiesLayout showing the Archivadas
    card in the left pane on /varieties/archived and beside an archived variety''s
    page.'
  version: 4
- subtask: integration
  location: web/e2e/archivadas.spec.ts:83
  claim: On a phone, the spec expects a link named "Rosa" after opening the archived
    page, but that page has only a heading "Rosa", so the step passes only when the
    Archivadas card has not finished unmounting yet. The declared web-e2e check fails
    at random on this head.
  version: 4

design_items:
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: 'Delete / Archive, step 2: VariedadDetalle and VariedadesCompu, the removal
    action'
  text: Sin prototipo. Neither variety page draws "Eliminar variedad" or "Archivar
    variedad". The only delete control is the per-price trash icon (aria-label "Borrar
    este precio"). The flow should say where the single removal action sits, likely
    below "En el catálogo", so that it is not confused with the per-price delete.
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: 'Delete, step 3: destructive ConfirmDialog (Estados "Antes de borrar")'
  text: The pattern exists in Estados ("¿Borrás la venta?", "No, dejala" / "Sí, borrala"),
    and the dialog to delete a variety can reuse it. The flow does not cite it, and
    does not say that the dialog's text must not echo the variety's name, which the
    Estados example's text does for a sale (date and amount).
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: 'Archive, step 3: archive confirmation with grams on hand'
  text: Sin prototipo. No confirmation in Estados shows the grams line. The flow follows
    the PRD, and the wording is left to design.
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: 'Archive, step 4: where the person lands after archiving'
  text: The flow sends the person back to Variedades after archiving. The PRD only
    says that delete goes back to Variedades and leaves archive's landing open. The
    flow's choice is reasonable, since staying would turn the page read-only under
    "‹ Variedades". It should state that this is a decision, so screens design does
    not read it as a slip.
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: 'Find & restore, step 1: "Archivadas (N)" at the foot of Variedades / VariedadesCompu'
  text: Sin prototipo. Both list screens draw only "Todas las entradas" at the foot
    (the phone Variedades file has it in two states). It is open how the new link
    stacks next to it on the phone and in the computer's left pane.
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: 'Find & restore, step 2: Archivadas card (phone and computer two panes)'
  text: 'Sin prototipo: no Archivadas screen exists. On a computer, the flow does
    not say whether "Restaurar" sits on each left-pane row, in the right pane, or
    in both.'
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: 'Find & restore, by the page: archived VariedadDetalle, read-only'
  text: Sin prototipo. The live page edits catalog attributes inline (Emoji, % índica,
    Aroma/sabor inputs and the "Mostrar en el catálogo" switch), not through an Editar
    form. The flow's list of controls that go away names the threshold switch and
    "Editar forms" but not these inline inputs or the catalog switch. The PRD (PM
    adjustment 4) says they are not editable.
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: 'Find & restore, by the page: "Ver todas" links on the archived page'
  text: VariedadDetalle links "Ver todas" to TodasLasEntradas and to VerTodas, the
    price history. The flow does not say whether the archived page keeps them. Keeping
    "Ver todas" for entries fits ADR-0009, since entries stay editable. VerTodas's
    per-price delete would have to be gone for an archived variety.
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: 'Find & restore, by the page: after "Restaurar" on the archived page'
  text: 'The flow ends the restore job at Archivadas, which fits the card path. For
    the page path it does not say where the person lands: does the page become the
    live page with "‹ Variedades", or does it go back to Archivadas?'
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: 'Find & restore, recovery: already restored by a partner'
  text: '"Eso ya no está. La lista ya está al día." for a variety a partner just restored
    is true of the Archivadas list, but it may read oddly. The recovery works: the
    list refreshes and the variety is in Variedades.'
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: 'Names: inline refusal on VariedadNueva (add / rename)'
  text: 'Sin prototipo: the inline refusal for a reserved name is not drawn. The recovery
    ("look in Archivadas") takes the person off the form, and the flow does not say
    whether the refusal links to Archivadas or whether typed input survives.'
- role_slug: ui_lead
  kind: observation
  step: VariedadDetalle (live) — removal action
  text: The style cited is wrong. 'Borrar este precio' is a neutral trash icon in
    the prototype and a 'Borrar' text in the built app, not a destructive text button.
    Build 'Eliminar variedad' from the Boton 'destructivo' variant (Componentes) or
    the ConfiguracionCategorias 'Eliminar' text link (#7A4A00). It must not look like
    the per-price delete.
- role_slug: ui_lead
  kind: observation
  step: VariedadDetalle (live) — removal action
  text: Build 'Archivar variedad' from the neutral 'suave' Boton, not the destructive
    one. Archiving loses nothing, so it should look calmer than 'Eliminar variedad'.
- role_slug: ui_lead
  kind: observation
  step: VariedadDetalle (live) — placement

    right pane on computer.
- role_slug: ui_lead
  kind: observation

    page starts from the built page and drops those 'Editar' links.

    only action is visible without scrolling.
- role_slug: ui_lead

    same 38px height for ''Restaurar''.'
- role_slug: ui_lead
  kind: observation

- role_slug: ui_lead

- role_slug: ui_lead
