# Variedades: delete a variety with no history, archive (and restore) one with history

## Original request

Blocked by: #47

### Problem

A variety can only be hidden from the catalog. A hidden variety still shows in Variedades (marked "Oculta"), in Nueva venta and everywhere else, so a variety loaded by mistake, or one the business no longer works with, can never leave the lists. The owner asked (2026-10-06): "¿No se pueden borrar variedades?"

### Decision (owner, 2026-10-06)

Two actions, chosen by whether the variety has history:

- **Delete** — only when the variety has no history at all: no sale lines, no stock entries or withdrawals, nothing else that references it (price entries alone don't count as history and go with it). The variety disappears for good. Confirm with a destructive ConfirmDialog.
- **Archive** — when it has history. The variety leaves every list and picker (Variedades' default list, Nueva venta, restock, catalog, pinned catalog), but every past sale, stock movement, price and Ganancia figure keeps its name and numbers unchanged. An archived variety can be **restored** and comes back exactly as it was.

Variedades offers a way to see the archived ones (e.g. a filter or a section "Archivadas") and restore them from there. Delete is only offered when it is possible; otherwise the action is Archive. The UI never offers a delete that the backend will refuse.

### Acceptance

- Deleting a variety with history is refused by the domain (not just hidden in the UI), with a test.
- An archived variety is absent from Nueva venta, restock, catalog text and pinned catalog; its past sales, Ganancia and stock history are unchanged (tests on results/analysis before and after archiving).
- Restore brings it back with its prices, stock and catalog attributes intact.
- Archive and hide are independent: archiving does not lose the hidden flag, and restoring brings it back as it was.
- docs/product/varieties.md documents both actions and when each applies.
- Works on phone and computer, light and dark, following the design system.

## PjM

New user-visible behaviour: delete and archive/restore actions for varieties, a new Archivadas view, and changes to the product definition in docs/product/varieties.md.

## Interview: Product Analyst→PM

### Round 1: questions

### Round 1: the job to be done

The `grilling` and `domain-modeling` skills are not available in this environment, so I am running the interview by hand in the same format. This round covers the job only. Behaviour questions come after you settle it.

What I found in the product knowledge:
- `varieties.md` says "Deleting a variety" is out of scope.
- The only way to take a variety out today is to hide it. A hidden variety still shows in Variedades, marked "Oculta".
- `CONTEXT.md` defines **Hidden variety** as "Hidden means only that". Hiding stays as it is; this request adds something separate.

❓ **Q1** - **Situation**: In which situation does the seller reach for this? The request names two: a variety loaded by mistake (a typo, a duplicate, a test), and one the business no longer works with (discontinued, out of season for good). Is there a third, for example a name that was reused or merged into another variety?
➡️ Recommendation: Two situations. (a) "I just created a variety by mistake and it has no history." (b) "I stopped selling a variety that has history and it keeps cluttering my lists." Nothing else.

❓ **Q2** - **Progress wanted**: What does the seller want to see once it is done? Is it that the variety is gone from every list and picker (Variedades, Nueva venta, restock, catalog, pinned catalog)? Or that past figures stay untouched? Or both?
➡️ Recommendation: Both. The lists show only what the seller still works with. The books never change: past sales, stock movements, prices and Ganancia keep their names and numbers. Where the seller wants it, the variety can come back exactly as it was.

❓ **Q3** - **How they judge success**: How will the seller know it went well?
➡️ Recommendation: Three criteria.
1. In a few taps from the phone, a variety leaves every list and picker. The app never offers a delete it will refuse.
2. After archiving, every past sale, stock figure and Ganancia and Análisis figure reads exactly as before, to the peso and the hundredth of a gram.
3. Restoring brings the variety back with its prices, stock and catalog attributes intact.

❓ **Q4** - **What they do today**: Without the feature, what does the seller do with an unwanted variety?
➡️ Recommendation: Take this as given from your quote ("¿No se pueden borrar variedades?"). They hide it from the catalog and live with it in the lists. Some rename it to something like "NO USAR" or leave it sold out. Say if you know of anything else, such as seeing a seller edit the spreadsheet.

❓ **Q5** - **The job story**: Do you accept this wording?
➡️ Recommendation: "When a variety I loaded by mistake, or one I no longer work with, keeps showing in my lists and pickers, I want to remove it, deleting it if it never moved and archiving it if it has history, so I can keep my lists clean without losing or changing any past sale, stock or profit figure, and bring it back if I start selling it again."

### Round 1: answers

**Q1**: Accepted. There are two situations and no more:
- (a) A variety created by mistake (a typo, a duplicate, a test) that never moved.
- (b) A variety the business no longer works with but that has history, and keeps cluttering the lists and pickers.

A reused or merged name is not a situation of this job. Merging varieties is out of scope. Whether an archived variety's name can be taken by a new one is a behaviour question for a later round, not part of the job.

**Q2**: Accepted. The seller wants both things at once:
- The lists and pickers show only what they still work with: Variedades' default list, Nueva venta, the stock entry form, the catalog text and the pinned catalog.
- The books never change. Every past sale, stock movement, price entry, Ganancia figure and Análisis figure keeps its variety name and its numbers.

An archived variety can come back exactly as it was. That includes its hidden flag, since archiving and hiding are separate. A deleted variety is gone for good, and that is fine because nothing ever referred to it.

**Q3**: Accepted, with three additions. The success criteria are:
1. **Out of the way in a few taps.** From the phone, and the same way on a computer, the seller takes a variety out of every list and picker from its own page. The app offers Delete only when the variety has no history and Archive otherwise. It never offers a delete the backend will refuse.
2. **The books don't move.** After archiving, every past sale and its lines, stock figure, cost of goods, stock shortfall, Ganancia, Caja and Análisis figure, and customer balance reads exactly as before, to the peso and the hundredth of a gram.
3. **Comes back whole.** Restoring brings the variety back with its prices, stock, entries, low-stock threshold, catalog attributes and hidden flag intact. It is back in every list and picker it qualifies for.

The three additions:
- The refusal to delete a variety with history is the domain's own rule, not only a hidden button.
- Archived varieties stay findable in Variedades. The seller can always see what they archived and restore it.
- The rules that apply everywhere still hold: the business stays isolated (ADR-0004), states are told by word and shape and never by colour alone, both themes are covered, and the toasts carry no data.

**Q4**: Accepted. Today the seller hides the variety from the catalog and lives with it in Variedades, marked "Oculta", and in Nueva venta. Some leave it sold out or rename it to something like "NO USAR".

Assumption: nothing else is known, and nothing else would change the PRD.

**Q5**: Accepted, with light rewording:

"When a variety I loaded by mistake, or one I no longer work with, keeps showing in my lists and pickers, I want to take it out from my phone in a few taps, deleting it if it never moved and archiving it if it has history, so my lists show only what I still sell, no past sale, stock, cost or profit figure changes, and I can bring it back exactly as it was if I start selling it again."

This is the navigation and varieties job refined for the end of a variety's life. It is not a new job.

### Round 2: questions

### Round 2: behaviour

The job is settled. Every question below is about behaviour that serves it.

Source note: `varieties.md` lists "Deleting a variety" as out of scope. This feature reverses that, so the PRD will replace that line. `CONTEXT.md` says "Hidden means only that"; archiving is a separate thing, and the PRD will add a glossary term for it.

❓ **Q1** - **What counts as history**: Delete is allowed only without history. You named sale lines and "stock entries or withdrawals". Does the rule use exactly these two, so a variety with any sale line (a gift included) or any stock entry (any origin, an adjustment or opening stock included) is archive-only? Do price entries, the low-stock threshold and catalog attributes go with a deleted variety? And if the seller later deletes the only sale or entry, does the variety become deletable?
➡️ Recommendation: Yes to all three.
- History is any sale line or any stock entry, read live and never stored.
- Prices, threshold and catalog attributes go with the delete.
- Deleting the last sale or entry makes Delete available again.
- The domain refuses a delete with history with its own coded refusal (ADR-0008), even if two partners race.

❓ **Q2** - **The name of an archived variety**: Variety names are unique per business, whatever the case or spacing. Can a new variety take the name of an archived one? A deleted variety's name is simply free.
➡️ Recommendation: No, the archived name stays reserved. Restore can then never collide with a newer variety. The refusal says the name belongs to an archived variety and points to Archivadas. The seller can rename the archived variety, or restore it, to change that. This keeps "restore brings it back exactly as it was" always true.

❓ **Q3** - **Archiving a variety that still has stock**: Can a variety with stock above zero, or with negative stock, be archived?
➡️ Recommendation: Yes, but never silently. The confirmation names the grams still on hand, e.g. "Te quedan 12 g. Si la archivás, dejan de verse en Inicio y en las listas, pero el stock queda guardado." Archiving never creates a stock entry or changes stock, lots or costs. An archived variety leaves Lo que te queda on Inicio, the Variedades summary line, the low-stock status and the pinned catalog's upkeep triggers. Archiving and restoring count as upkeep triggers, since the catalog text may change.

❓ **Q4** - **What archived varieties still show in**: The books must not move. Do these keep showing the variety by its plain name, with no change of figures: past sales and their detail, Todas las entradas, Análisis' margin rows and month breakdowns, and the customer account? And may the seller still edit or delete a stock entry that belongs to an archived variety, while the archived variety is not offered in any picker for new records?
➡️ Recommendation: Yes. Past records show the name as before, with no "archivada" tag, because a tag would change what a past sale shows. Existing entries stay editable (ADR-0009: an entry is never frozen), except that the variety field of an entry edit cannot be moved onto an archived variety. A new sale line, stock entry or price entry cannot be recorded against an archived variety. If a partner archives a variety while a form is open, saving is refused as "Eso ya no está", following `accounts.md`.

❓ **Q5** - **Where Archivadas lives and what its page allows**: Where does the seller see and restore archived varieties, and what can they do on an archived variety's page?
➡️ Recommendation:
- Variedades' default list excludes archived varieties.
- Below the list, next to "Todas las entradas", a link "Archivadas (N)" opens a card ("‹ Variedades") listing them by name with the word "Archivada", each with "Restaurar". With none, the link is left out.
- Opening one shows its page read-only: no "+ Entrada", no "Nuevo precio" and no edit forms, with "Restaurar" as its one action.
- Restore is one tap and shows the toast "Variedad restaurada", with no confirmation, since nothing is lost.
- The variety's own page offers exactly one removal action: "Eliminar variedad" (destructive ConfirmDialog, toast "Variedad eliminada") when it has no history, otherwise "Archivar variedad" (toast "Variedad archivada").
- It works on the two-pane computer layout as well. Archiving or deleting the selected variety selects the next one by name.

### Round 2: answers

**Q1**: Accepted.
- **History** is any sale line of the variety (gifts and special prices included) or any stock entry of it (any origin: purchase, harvest, opening stock, or an adjustment of either sign). It is worked out live and never stored.
- **Not history**: price entries, the low-stock threshold and catalog attributes. They are deleted together with the variety.
- **Becomes deletable again**: once the seller deletes the variety's last sale or last stock entry, Delete is offered again.
- **The rule belongs to the domain**: a delete with history is refused with its own coded refusal in `contract.json` (ADR-0008), which repeats no name or amount. The check and the delete are one guarded write, so a sale or entry a partner records at the same moment either blocks the delete or is refused as "Eso ya no está". Nothing is ever orphaned.
- **Deleting a gone variety** follows accounts.md's "Eso ya no está. La lista ya está al día."

**Q2**: Accepted. An archived variety keeps its name reserved under the existing uniqueness rule (case and spacing ignored).
- Adding or renaming a variety to that name is refused inline with its own fixed refusal: the name belongs to an archived variety, look in Archivadas. Variety names are not personal data, but the refusal still repeats nothing typed (ADR-0005, ADR-0008).
- An archived variety can be renamed only after it is restored: its page is read-only (Q5). Restore therefore never collides.
- A deleted variety's name is free at once.

**Q3**: Accepted. A variety with stock above zero, at zero or below zero can be archived. A confirmation always comes first.
- **The confirmation** names the grams on hand when stock is not zero. With zero stock it simply asks. The exact wording is design's. Like every "Antes de borrar" dialog, it may name the variety and grams, because it is not a toast.
- **Nothing moves**: archiving and restoring never create a stock entry and never change stock, lots, cost of goods, pending cost, prices or any figure.
- **What an archived variety leaves**:
  - Variedades' list and its summary line;
  - Inicio's Lo que te queda;
  - the stock entry form's variety picker;
  - Nueva venta's variety picker;
  - the price entry form;
  - the catalog text and the catalog screen's "what does not go out" list;
  - the pinned catalog.
- **Upkeep**: archiving and restoring are upkeep triggers (pinned-catalog.md), because they can change the catalog text.

Assumption: an archived variety does not count for "Arrancá por las variedades" on Inicio, which shows when no unarchived variety has a current price today. It does not count for the first-run checklist either, but that checklist never returns once gone, so this changes nothing in practice.

**Q4**: Accepted, with one addition.
- **Past records show the plain name with no "archivada" tag**, and their figures do not change. This covers:
  - Ventas and a sale's detail;
  - the customer's account;
  - Todas las entradas;
  - Análisis' variety rows and every week, month and since-the-start figure;
  - Results' Ganancia and Caja;
  - the business export.
- **Existing records stay fully workable** (ADR-0009):
  - a stock entry of an archived variety can be edited or deleted from Todas las entradas;
  - a sale containing one can be deleted, Deshacer included;
  - Asignar cliente still works.
- **The one limit on edits**: an entry edit can keep the archived variety it already has, but can never move an entry onto a different archived variety.
- **Nothing new against an archived variety**: no new sale line, stock entry or price entry. A form open while a partner archives the variety is refused on save following accounts.md's "Eso ya no está" rule, and keeps the rest of what was typed where the screen allows. In Nueva venta, the line shows the generic gone message and the form stays open (sales.md's existing rule for a variety not found).

**Q5**: Accepted, with these changes.
- **Variedades' list** leaves archived varieties out. "Archivadas (N)" sits at the foot next to "Todas las entradas", and is left out when there are none.
- **The Archivadas card** ("‹ Variedades") lists the archived varieties by name, each marked "Archivada" in words, each with "Restaurar".
- **An archived variety's page** is a detail with the back arrow "‹ Archivadas".
  - Its summary line and sections show read-only, "Archivada" in words included.
  - It has no "+ Entrada", "Nuevo precio", price delete, threshold switch, rename, Editar forms or delete. Its one action is "Restaurar".
  - To delete an archived variety that has lost all its history, the seller restores it first, then deletes it. No second path.
- **Restore** is one tap, with no confirmation, and shows the toast "Variedad restaurada". The variety returns to the list with its hidden flag, prices, stock, entries, threshold and catalog attributes exactly as they were.
- **A live variety's page** shows exactly one removal action, worked out from history when the page is read:
  - with no history: "Eliminar variedad", through a destructive ConfirmDialog, then the toast "Variedad eliminada" and back to Variedades;
  - with history: "Archivar variedad", with the Q3 confirmation, then the toast "Variedad archivada".

  Toasts carry no data.
- **Stale page**: if history appears after the page loaded (a partner sold it), the delete is refused with the Q1 refusal and the page refreshes to show Archivar. The UI never offers a delete it knows will be refused.
- **Computer**: on the two-pane layout, archiving or deleting the selected variety selects the next one by name, else the previous one, else the empty state. Archivadas uses the same two panes.
- **Everywhere**: phone and computer, light and dark, states by word and shape, never by colour alone.
- **Isolation**: every new route joins the isolation sweep and the gated-route test (ADR-0004).

Assumption: the business export holds the archived flag as a stored fact, and the import treats an archived variety as a record like any other. Neither changes the PRD's scope.

### Closing

### Closing summary

The PM settled the job and all four behaviour questions of round 2. No question is left that could change the PRD.

**Delete and archive (Q1)**
- **History** is any sale line or any stock entry of the variety, whatever its origin. It is read live and never stored.
- A variety with no history can be deleted. Its prices, threshold and catalog attributes go with it.
- Once the last sale or entry is deleted, Delete is offered again.
- The domain refuses a delete with history, with its own coded refusal in `contract.json` (ADR-0008).
- The check and the delete are one guarded write, so a partner's concurrent record cannot orphan anything.
- A delete of a variety that is already gone gets "Eso ya no está".

**Names (Q2)**
- An archived variety keeps its name reserved under the existing uniqueness rule (case and spacing ignored).
- A new or renamed variety that takes that name is refused with a fixed refusal that points to Archivadas. It repeats nothing typed.
- A deleted variety's name is free at once.

**Archiving with stock (Q3)**
- A variety with positive, zero or negative stock can be archived, always after a confirmation. When stock is not zero, the confirmation names the grams on hand.
- Archiving and restoring change no stock, lot, cost, price or other figure.
- An archived variety leaves:
  - Variedades' list and summary line;
  - Inicio's Lo que te queda;
  - the pickers for new sales, stock entries and price entries;
  - the catalog text, the "what does not go out" list and the pinned catalog.
- Archiving and restoring are upkeep triggers for the pinned catalog.
- An archived variety does not count for "Arrancá por las variedades" on Inicio.

**Past records (Q4)**
- Past records show the plain name, with no "archivada" tag, and their figures do not change. This covers:
  - sales and sale detail;
  - the customer account;
  - Todas las entradas;
  - Análisis;
  - Results;
  - the export.
- Existing records stay workable: edit and delete of entries, delete and Deshacer of sales, and Asignar cliente.
- An entry edit keeps its variety but can never move onto a different archived one.
- Nothing new can be recorded against an archived variety. A form left open while a partner archives is refused with "Eso ya no está", and keeps what was typed where the screen allows.

**Screens (Q5)**
- Variedades' list leaves archived varieties out. "Archivadas (N)" sits at the foot next to "Todas las entradas" and is left out when there are none.
- The Archivadas card lists archived varieties, each marked "Archivada" in words and each with "Restaurar".
- An archived variety's page is read-only and its one action is "Restaurar".
- Restore is one tap, with no confirmation, and shows the toast "Variedad restaurada". The variety returns exactly as it was.
- A live variety's page offers exactly one removal action:
  - with no history, "Eliminar variedad" (destructive ConfirmDialog, toast "Variedad eliminada");
  - with history, "Archivar variedad" (toast "Variedad archivada").
- A stale page that gets a refused delete refreshes to show Archivar.
- On the two-pane layout, archiving or deleting the selected variety selects the next one by name, else the previous one, else the empty state.
- It works on phone and computer, in light and dark, with states shown by word and shape.
- New routes join the isolation sweep and the gated-route test (ADR-0004).

**For the PRD**
- `varieties.md`'s "Deleting a variety" out-of-scope line is reversed.
- The glossary gains an "archived" term, separate from "hidden", which `CONTEXT.md` says means only that.
- Assumption: the export holds the archived flag as a stored fact, and the import treats an archived variety like any other record.

## Product Analyst

### Problem

A variety can only be hidden from the catalog today. A hidden variety still shows in Variedades (marked "Oculta"), in Nueva venta and in every other list. A variety loaded by mistake, or one the business no longer works with, can never leave the lists. Today the seller hides it and lives with it, renames it to something like "NO USAR", or leaves it sold out. The owner asked (2026-10-06): "¿No se pueden borrar variedades?"

### Job story

When a variety I loaded by mistake, or one I no longer work with, keeps showing in my lists and pickers, I want to take it out from my phone in a few taps, deleting it if it never moved and archiving it if it has history, so my lists show only what I still sell, no past sale, stock, cost or profit figure changes, and I can bring it back exactly as it was if I start selling it again.

This is the varieties and navigation job applied to the end of a variety's life. It is not a new job.

### Success criteria

1. **Out of the way in a few taps.** From a variety's own page, on phone and computer, the seller takes it out of every list and picker. The app offers Delete only when the variety has no history, and Archive otherwise. It never offers a delete that will be refused.
2. **The books don't move.** After archiving, every past sale and its lines, stock figure, cost of goods, stock shortfall, Ganancia, Caja, Análisis figure and customer balance reads exactly as before, to the peso and the hundredth of a gram.
3. **Comes back whole.** Restoring brings the variety back with its prices, stock, entries, low-stock threshold, catalog attributes and hidden flag intact. It is back in every list and picker it qualifies for.
4. **The domain decides.** A delete of a variety with history is refused by the domain itself, not only hidden in the UI.
5. **Archived varieties stay findable.** The seller can always see what they archived and restore it.
6. **Nothing new in the rules that already hold.** Businesses stay isolated (ADR-0004). Every state is told by word and shape, never by colour alone, in both themes. Toasts carry no data.

### Behaviour

#### Two actions, chosen by history

- **History** is any sale line of the variety (gifts and special prices included) or any stock entry of it (any origin: purchase, harvest, opening stock, or an adjustment of either sign). It is worked out live and never stored.
- **Not history**: price entries, the low-stock threshold and catalog attributes. A deleted variety takes them with it.
- **Delete**: offered only for a variety with no history. The variety disappears for good.
- **Archive**: offered for a variety with history. The variety leaves every list and picker but stays in the books.
- **Delete becomes available again** once the seller deletes the variety's last sale or last stock entry.
- **Refusal**: a delete with history is refused by the domain with its own coded refusal in `contract.json` (ADR-0008). It repeats no name or amount (ADR-0005).
- **The check and the delete are one guarded write.** A sale or entry a partner records at the same moment either blocks the delete or is refused as "Eso ya no está". Nothing is ever orphaned.
- **A variety already gone** answers 404 and follows accounts.md: "Eso ya no está. La lista ya está al día."

#### Archiving

- A variety with stock above, at or below zero can be archived. A confirmation always comes first.
- When stock is not zero, the confirmation names the grams on hand, for example "Te quedan 12 g. Si la archivás, dejan de verse en Inicio y en las listas, pero el stock queda guardado." With zero stock it simply asks. The exact wording is design's.
- Archiving and restoring never create a stock entry and never change stock, lots, cost of goods, pending cost, prices or any other figure.
- **An archived variety leaves**:
  - Variedades' list and its summary line;
  - Inicio's Lo que te queda;
  - Nueva venta's variety picker;
  - the stock entry form's variety picker;
  - the price entry form;
  - the catalog text and the catalog screen's "what does not go out" list;
  - the pinned catalog.
- It does not count for "Arrancá por las variedades" on Inicio. That notice shows when no unarchived variety has a current price today.
- **Upkeep**: archiving and restoring are upkeep triggers (pinned-catalog.md), because they can change the catalog text.
- **Archive and hide are independent.** Archiving keeps the hidden flag, and restoring brings it back as it was.

#### Names

- An archived variety keeps its name reserved under the existing uniqueness rule (case and spacing ignored).
- Adding or renaming a variety to that name is refused inline with its own fixed refusal: the name belongs to an archived variety, look in Archivadas. It repeats nothing typed.
- An archived variety can be renamed only after it is restored, so restore never collides.
- A deleted variety's name is free at once.

#### What archived varieties still show in

- **Past records show the plain name with no "archivada" tag, and their figures do not change.** This covers:
  - Ventas and a sale's detail;
  - the customer's account;
  - Todas las entradas;
  - Análisis' variety rows and every week, month and since-the-start figure;
  - Results' Ganancia and Caja;
  - the business export.
- **Existing records stay workable** (ADR-0009):
  - a stock entry of an archived variety can be edited or deleted from Todas las entradas;
  - a sale containing one can be deleted, Deshacer included;
  - Asignar cliente still works.
- An entry edit can keep the archived variety it already has, but can never move the entry onto a different archived one.
- **Nothing new is recorded against an archived variety**: no sale line, stock entry or price entry.
- A form left open while a partner archives the variety is refused on save with accounts.md's "Eso ya no está" rule, and keeps the rest of what was typed where the screen allows. In Nueva venta the line shows the generic gone message and the form stays open (sales.md's rule for a variety not found).

#### On screen

- **Variedades' list** leaves archived varieties out. "Archivadas (N)" sits at the foot next to "Todas las entradas", and is left out when there are none.
- **The Archivadas card** ("‹ Variedades") lists archived varieties by name, each marked "Archivada" in words, each with "Restaurar".
- **An archived variety's page** is a detail with the back arrow "‹ Archivadas".
  - Its summary line and sections are read-only, "Archivada" in words included.
  - It has no "+ Entrada", "Nuevo precio", price delete, threshold switch, rename, Editar forms or delete.
  - Its one action is "Restaurar".
  - To delete an archived variety that has lost all its history, the seller restores it first, then deletes it. There is no second path.
- **Restore** is one tap, with no confirmation, and shows the toast "Variedad restaurada". The variety returns to the list exactly as it was.
- **A live variety's page** shows exactly one removal action, worked out from history when the page is read:
  - with no history: "Eliminar variedad", through a destructive ConfirmDialog, then the toast "Variedad eliminada" and back to Variedades;
  - with history: "Archivar variedad", through the confirmation above, then the toast "Variedad archivada".
- **Stale page**: if history appears after the page loaded, the delete is refused with the coded refusal and the page refreshes to show "Archivar variedad".
- **Computer**: on the two-pane layout, archiving or deleting the selected variety selects the next one by name, else the previous one, else the empty state. Archivadas uses the same two panes.
- Everything works on phone and computer, in light and dark, following the design system.

#### Isolation

Every new route joins the isolation sweep and the gated-route test (ADR-0004). Another business's variety, archived or not, answers 404.

### Product documents to change

- `docs/product/varieties.md`: replace the out-of-scope line "Deleting a variety" with both actions and when each applies, the Archivadas view, and the new screens and routes.
- `CONTEXT.md`: add **Archived variety**, separate from **Hidden variety**, which stays "hidden means only that". Hidden's _Avoid_ list names "archived", so that line is revised.
- Where a document lists what a screen or the catalog shows, say that archived varieties are left out: `catalog.md`, `pinned-catalog.md` (upkeep triggers), `results.md` (Lo que te queda), `sales.md` and `stock.md` (pickers).
- `scripts/design/screens.json` and `pantallas/` gain the Archivadas card and the archived variety's page, as `navigation.md` requires.

### Assumptions

- The business export holds the archived flag as a stored fact, and the import treats an archived variety like any other record.
- The first-run checklist is unaffected in practice, since it never returns once gone.

### Out of scope

- Merging varieties, or taking over an archived variety's name.
- Deleting or archiving several varieties at once.
- An "archivada" tag on past records.
- Deleting a variety with history, by any path.
- Moving a stock entry onto an archived variety.
- Live updates between partners while a screen is open.

## PM (v1)

### Verdict

**approved**: the PRD matches every interview decision (history, names, archiving with stock, past records, screens) and serves the settled job. The gaps were small enough for me to close myself.

### Adjustments

1. **Metrics were missing.** The PRD had no Metrics section, and the job's success criteria were not measured. I added one metric per criterion: the removal-action matrix, the race, books unchanged before and after, out of every list and picker, restore whole, names, nothing new recorded, isolation, no curl, taps and design.
2. **Routes and back.** The PRD did not give paths for the two new screens, and navigation.md requires every screen to have one.
   - The Archivadas card is at `/varieties/archived`.
   - An archived variety's page stays at `/varieties/{low}`, the same page as a live variety, read-only.
   - With no history to go back to, an archived variety's page goes back to Archivadas.
3. **Computer auto-selection.** Opening Variedades with nothing selected now selects the first unarchived variety by name. Opening Archivadas with nothing selected selects the first archived one.
4. **Catalog attributes of an archived variety** are not editable. This follows the read-only page from Q5, and catalog.md now says so.
5. **Documents to change.** navigation.md is added to the list, since its routes and chrome list must name the new screens.
   - catalog.md, pinned-catalog.md, results.md and navigation.md are updated where their text would otherwise contradict this feature.
   - sales.md and stock.md have no contradicting line. varieties.md now carries the rule that archived varieties are offered in no picker for new records, and is the reference for it.
6. **Out of scope** gains two lines: archiving or deleting from the list itself, and restoring several at once.

### Validated flags

- `touches_ui`: **true**. There are new actions on a variety's page, a new Archivadas card and a read-only archived page, and the lists change.
- `touches_copy`: **true**. There are new buttons, confirmations, toasts and refusals.

### PRD

#### Problem

Today a variety can only be hidden from the catalog. A hidden variety still shows in Variedades (marked "Oculta"), in Nueva venta and in every other list. So a variety loaded by mistake, or one the business no longer works with, can never leave the lists. Sellers hide it and live with it, rename it to something like "NO USAR", or leave it sold out. The owner asked (2026-10-06): "¿No se pueden borrar variedades?"

#### Job story

When a variety I loaded by mistake, or one I no longer work with, keeps showing in my lists and pickers, I want to take it out from my phone in a few taps, deleting it if it never moved and archiving it if it has history, so my lists show only what I still sell, no past sale, stock, cost or profit figure changes, and I can bring it back exactly as it was if I start selling it again.

This applies the varieties and navigation job to the end of a variety's life. It is not a new job.

#### Success criteria

1. **Out of the way in a few taps.** From a variety's own page, on phone and computer, the seller takes it out of every list and picker. The app offers Delete only when the variety has no history, and Archive otherwise. It never offers a delete that will be refused.
2. **The books don't move.** After archiving, each of these reads exactly as before, to the peso and the hundredth of a gram:
   - every past sale and its lines;
   - stock figures, cost of goods and stock shortfall;
   - Ganancia, Caja and every Análisis figure;
   - customer balances.
3. **Comes back whole.** Restoring brings the variety back with its prices, stock, entries, low-stock threshold, catalog attributes and hidden flag intact. It is back in every list and picker it qualifies for.
4. **The domain decides.** The domain itself refuses to delete a variety with history; hiding the button in the UI is not enough.
5. **Archived varieties stay findable.** The seller can always see what they archived and restore it.
6. **The rules that already hold still hold.** Businesses stay isolated (ADR-0004). Every state is told by word and shape, never by colour alone, in both themes. Toasts carry no data.

#### Behaviour

##### Two actions, chosen by history

- **History** is any sale line of the variety, or any stock entry of it.
  - Sale lines include gifts and special prices.
  - Stock entries of any origin count: purchase, harvest, opening stock, or an adjustment of either sign.
  - History is worked out live and never stored.
- **Not history**: price entries, the low-stock threshold and catalog attributes. A deleted variety takes them with it.
- **Delete**: offered only for a variety with no history. The variety disappears for good.
- **Archive**: offered for a variety with history. The variety leaves every list and picker but stays in the books.
- **Delete becomes available again** once the seller deletes the variety's last sale or last stock entry.
- **Refusal**: the domain refuses a delete with history, with its own coded refusal in `contract.json` (ADR-0008). The refusal repeats no name or amount (ADR-0005).
- **The check and the delete are one guarded write.** A sale or entry a partner records at the same moment either blocks the delete or is refused as "Eso ya no está". Nothing is ever orphaned.
- **A variety already gone** answers 404 and follows accounts.md: "Eso ya no está. La lista ya está al día."

##### Archiving

- A variety can be archived whether its stock is above, at or below zero. A confirmation always comes first.
- When stock is not zero, the confirmation names the grams on hand. For example: "Te quedan 12 g. Si la archivás, dejan de verse en Inicio y en las listas, pero el stock queda guardado." With zero stock it simply asks. The exact wording is design's.
- Archiving and restoring never create a stock entry. They never change stock, lots, cost of goods, pending cost, prices or any other figure.
- **An archived variety leaves**:
  - Variedades' list and its summary line;
  - Inicio's Lo que te queda;
  - Nueva venta's variety picker;
  - the stock entry form's variety picker;
  - the price entry form;
  - the catalog text and the catalog screen's "what does not go out" list;
  - the pinned catalog.
- **"Arrancá por las variedades"** on Inicio does not count it. That notice shows when no unarchived variety has a current price today.
- **Upkeep**: archiving and restoring are upkeep triggers (pinned-catalog.md), because they can change the catalog text.
- **Archive and hide are independent.** Archiving keeps the hidden flag, and restoring brings it back as it was.

##### Names

- An archived variety keeps its name reserved under the existing uniqueness rule (case and spacing ignored).
- Adding a variety with that name, or renaming one to it, is refused inline with its own fixed refusal: the name belongs to an archived variety, look in Archivadas. The refusal repeats nothing typed.
- An archived variety can be renamed only after it is restored, so restoring never collides.
- A deleted variety's name is free at once.

##### What archived varieties still show in

- **Past records show the plain name, with no "archivada" tag, and their figures do not change.** This covers:
  - Ventas and a sale's detail;
  - the customer's account;
  - Todas las entradas;
  - Análisis' variety rows and every week, month and since-the-start figure;
  - Results' Ganancia and Caja;
  - the business export.
- **Existing records stay workable** (ADR-0009):
  - a stock entry of an archived variety can be edited or deleted from Todas las entradas;
  - a sale containing one can be deleted, Deshacer included;
  - Asignar cliente still works.
- **Entry edits**: an edit can keep the archived variety the entry already has, but can never move the entry onto a different archived variety.
- **Nothing new is recorded against an archived variety**: no sale line, stock entry or price entry. A reference to one for a new record answers as gone.
- **A form left open while a partner archives the variety** is refused on save under accounts.md's "Eso ya no está" rule. It keeps the rest of what was typed where the screen allows. In Nueva venta, the line shows the generic gone message and the form stays open (sales.md's rule for a variety not found).

##### On screen

- **Variedades' list** leaves archived varieties out. "Archivadas (N)" sits at the foot, next to "Todas las entradas". It is left out when there are none.
- **The Archivadas card** (`/varieties/archived`, "‹ Variedades") lists the archived varieties by name. Each is marked "Archivada" in words and has "Restaurar".
- **An archived variety's page** (`/varieties/{low}`, the same page, read-only) is a detail with the back arrow "‹ Archivadas". With no history to go back to, back goes to Archivadas.
  - Its summary line and sections are read-only, and "Archivada" shows in words.
  - It has none of these: "+ Entrada", "Nuevo precio", price delete, the threshold switch, rename, the Editar forms or delete.
  - Its one action is "Restaurar".
  - To delete an archived variety that has lost all its history, the seller restores it first, then deletes it. There is no second path.
- **Restore** is one tap with no confirmation. It shows the toast "Variedad restaurada", and the variety returns to the list exactly as it was.
- **A live variety's page** shows exactly one removal action, worked out from history when the page is read:
  - with no history: "Eliminar variedad", through a destructive ConfirmDialog, then the toast "Variedad eliminada" and back to Variedades;
  - with history: "Archivar variedad", through the confirmation above, then the toast "Variedad archivada".
- **Stale page**: if history appears after the page loaded, the delete is refused with the coded refusal, and the page refreshes to show "Archivar variedad".
- **Computer**:
  - Opening Variedades with nothing selected selects the first unarchived variety by name.
  - Archiving or deleting the selected variety selects the next one by name, else the previous one, else the empty state.
  - Archivadas uses the same two panes and selects the first archived variety by name.
- Everything works on phone and computer, in light and dark, following the design system.

##### Isolation

Every new route joins the isolation sweep and the gated-route test (ADR-0004). Another business's variety, archived or not, answers 404.

#### Metrics

- **Offered action (criteria 1, 4)**, a matrix that must pass 100%:
  - The page offers "Eliminar variedad" exactly when the variety has no history, and "Archivar variedad" otherwise.
  - History is tested from each source alone: a list line, a gift, a special price, and an entry of each origin, both adjustment signs included.
  - Price entries, a threshold and catalog attributes alone leave Delete offered.
  - Deleting the last sale or entry offers Delete again.
  - The domain refuses a delete with history with its code, through the API, whatever the UI showed.
- **No orphan (criterion 4)**: a repository test over real SQLite races a delete against a sale line and against a stock entry. Either the delete is refused, or the record is refused as gone. There are 0 rows referencing a missing variety.
- **Books unchanged (criterion 2)**:
  - Over a seeded history, these are captured before archiving, after archiving and after restoring:
    - `GET /analysis` for every week, month and the all-time window;
    - `GET /panel`'s period figures;
    - every sale's detail;
    - every customer's balance and account;
    - stock, cost of goods and pending grams per variety.
  - The three captures differ in 0 figures.
- **Out of every list and picker (criterion 1)**: front and API tests show an archived variety absent from:
  - Variedades' list and summary line;
  - Lo que te queda;
  - Nueva venta's, the stock entry form's and the price form's choices;
  - the catalog text and its "what does not go out" list;
  - the pinned catalog's text.

  Upkeep edits the pin on archive and on restore exactly when the text changes. 100% passing.
- **Comes back whole (criterion 3)**: after a restore, the variety's prices, stock, entries, threshold, catalog attributes and hidden flag (tested hidden and shown) equal what they were before archiving. 100% passing.
- **Names**: an archived variety's name, in any case or spacing, is refused on add and on rename with its own code. A deleted variety's name is accepted at once.
- **Nothing new**: a new sale line, stock entry or price entry against an archived variety is refused as gone. An entry edit keeps its own archived variety, and is refused when it moves onto another archived one. Editing and deleting that entry, deleting a sale containing it (Deshacer included) and Asignar cliente all still work.
- **Findable (criterion 5)**: "Archivadas (N)" shows the right count and is absent with none. Restore works in 1 tap from the card and from the archived page. The archived page offers no action but "Restaurar".
- **Taps (criterion 1)**: through the front on a phone viewport, archiving or deleting takes the action plus its confirmation from the variety's page, and the computer takes no more.
- **Isolation and no echo (criterion 6)**:
  - Every new route is in the sweep and the gated-route test, and answers 404 from another business.
  - No refusal or toast carries a name or an amount.
- **No curl**: the end-to-end front run:
  - deletes a fresh variety;
  - archives one with a sale;
  - finds it in Archivadas;
  - opens its read-only page;
  - restores it.
- **Design (criterion 6)**: `scripts/design/compare.sh` runs before and after for the Variedades list, the variety page, Archivadas and the archived page, on phone and computer, in both themes, attached to the PR.

#### Product documents to change

- `docs/product/varieties.md`: both actions, history, names, Archivadas, the archived page, routes and metrics. The "Deleting a variety" out-of-scope line is reversed.
- `CONTEXT.md`: **Archived variety** and **Variety history** are new. **Hidden variety**, **Variety** and **Catalog** are revised.
- `catalog.md` (who qualifies, what does not go out, attributes), `pinned-catalog.md` (upkeep triggers), `results.md` (Lo que te queda, "Arrancá por las variedades") and `navigation.md` (routes, chrome, two panes).
- `scripts/design/screens.json` and `pantallas/` gain the Archivadas card and the archived variety's page.

#### Assumptions

- The business export holds the archived flag as a stored fact, and the import treats an archived variety like any other record.
- In practice, the first-run checklist is unaffected, since it never returns once gone.

#### Out of scope

- Merging varieties, or taking over an archived variety's name.
- Deleting or archiving several varieties at once; restoring several at once.
- Archiving or deleting from the list itself rather than from the variety's page.
- An "archivada" tag on past records.
- Deleting a variety with history, by any path.
- Moving a stock entry onto an archived variety.
- Live updates between partners while a screen is open.

### For the next role

The Tech Lead needs to know:

- **Routes and ADR-0030**:
  - `/varieties/archived` is a new exact shared front path. Add it to `SHARED_FRONT_PATHS` and `contract.json`'s `sharedFrontRoutes`, together with its front route.
  - The archived page reuses `/varieties/{low}`, which `^/varieties/[0-9]+$` already covers.
- **The guarded delete** must be one statement or one transaction that checks for no sale line and no stock entry of the variety. Under pysqlite's legacy mode, a read-then-delete is not enough (ADR-0012, ADR-0019 set the precedent).
- **New refusal codes** for `contract.json`, each with its kind:
  - a delete with history (refusal);
  - a name reserved by an archived variety (refusal);
  - an archived variety referenced for a new record, which answers as the existing gone code or a new one of kind `gone`.
- **History is computed on read.** The archived flag is the only new stored fact. The business export picks it up through ADR-0015's reflection with no change to the command.
- **Who filters archived varieties out**, read through ports (ADR-0006):
  - the default `GET /varieties`;
  - the catalog's qualifying set and its "does not go out" list;
  - results' stock rows and "Arrancá por las variedades".

  Period figures (ADR-0020's scan), sale views, accounts and Todas las entradas must not filter them.
- **Pinned catalog**: archive and restore join the upkeep triggers, on the varieties router's writes (ADR-0014).
- **Tests to plan**: the isolation sweep and the gated-route test cover every new route. The before/after agreement test is the key proof of criterion 2.

- `docs/product/varieties.md`: both actions, history, names, Archivadas, the archived page, routes and metrics. The "Deleting a variety" out-of-scope line is reversed.
- `CONTEXT.md`: **Archived variety** and **Variety history** are new. **Hidden variety**, **Variety** and **Catalog** are revised.
- `catalog.md` (who qualifies, what does not go out, attributes), `pinned-catalog.md` (upkeep triggers), `results.md` (Lo que te queda, "Arrancá por las variedades") and `navigation.md` (routes, chrome, two panes).
- `scripts/design/screens.json` and `pantallas/` gain the Archivadas card and the archived variety's page.

#### Assumptions

- The business export holds the archived flag as a stored fact, and the import treats an archived variety like any other record.
- In practice, the first-run checklist is unaffected, since it never returns once gone.

#### Out of scope

- Merging varieties, or taking over an archived variety's name.
- Deleting or archiving several varieties at once; restoring several at once.
- Archiving or deleting from the list itself rather than from the variety's page.
- An "archivada" tag on past records.
- Deleting a variety with history, by any path.
- Moving a stock entry onto an archived variety.
- Live updates between partners while a screen is open.

### For the next role

The Tech Lead needs to know:

- **Routes and ADR-0030**:
  - `/varieties/archived` is a new exact shared front path. Add it to `SHARED_FRONT_PATHS` and `contract.json`'s `sharedFrontRoutes`, together with its front route.
  - The archived page reuses `/varieties/{low}`, which `^/varieties/[0-9]+$` already covers.
- **The guarded delete** must be one statement or one transaction that checks for no sale line and no stock entry of the variety. Under pysqlite's legacy mode, a read-then-delete is not enough (ADR-0012, ADR-0019 set the precedent).
- **New refusal codes** for `contract.json`, each with its kind:
  - a delete with history (refusal);
  - a name reserved by an archived variety (refusal);
  - an archived variety referenced for a new record, which answers as the existing gone code or a new one of kind `gone`.
- **History is computed on read.** The archived flag is the only new stored fact. The business export picks it up through ADR-0015's reflection with no change to the command.
- **Who filters archived varieties out**, read through ports (ADR-0006):
  - the default `GET /varieties`;
  - the catalog's qualifying set and its "does not go out" list;
  - results' stock rows and "Arrancá por las variedades".

  Period figures (ADR-0020's scan), sale views, accounts and Todas las entradas must not filter them.
- **Pinned catalog**: archive and restore join the upkeep triggers, on the varieties router's writes (ADR-0014).
- **Tests to plan**: the isolation sweep and the gated-route test cover every new route. The before/after agreement test is the key proof of criterion 2.

### Clarifications (round 3)

### Answers to the Tech Lead (round 3)

**Q1: archiving a variety whose history disappeared after the page loaded.** The answer is **(b)**: the app archives it anyway.

- **Why.** The seller tapped Archivar to get the variety out of their lists, and archiving does that whether or not the variety still has history.
  - Archiving loses nothing, and it can always be undone with Restaurar.
  - Refusing it would add a refusal and a refresh that protect nothing.
  - An archived variety with no history can already happen. A seller can delete an archived variety's last stock entry from Todas las entradas, or its last sale. The interview settled that case: restore first, then delete. So (b) adds no new state.
- **The rule, made precise.**
  - History decides which removal action the page *offers*. It is worked out when the page is read.
  - Only **delete** is guarded by the domain.
  - **Archive** is never refused because of history, whether there is some or none.
  - "The app never offers a delete that will be refused" still holds. The only refusal a seller can meet is the stale-page delete, which already refreshes to show "Archivar variedad".
- **Nothing else changes.**
  - A variety already gone still answers "Eso ya no está".
  - Archiving a variety that is already archived is a no-op.
  - Restoring brings it back. Its page then offers "Eliminar variedad", because it has no history.

`varieties.md` is updated to say this. The glossary's **Archived variety** no longer says it always has history.

## UX Designer

### Flow

Design data checked: `pantallas/` has the Variedades list (phone and computer), the variety page (`VariedadDetalle`) and the new-variety form. A search of `pantallas/` for "Archiv", "Eliminar variedad" and "Restaurar" found nothing. So no screen draws a removal action, the Archivadas card, or an archived variety's page. Where a step below depends on one of these, it is marked **[no screen drawn]**. I describe the flow from the PRD and invent no layout.

### Flow: Delete a variety that never moved

- **Starts at:** Variedades, a tab root (phone: list; computer: two panes).
- **Steps:**
  1. Open the variety. On a phone, tap the row to reach the variety's page (`‹ Variedades`). On a computer, the first unarchived variety is already selected, so reaching another one takes one click in the left pane.
  2. On the variety's page, tap "Eliminar variedad". With no history, this is the only removal action shown. **[no screen drawn]**
  3. A destructive ConfirmDialog asks. Confirm.
  4. The person returns to Variedades. The toast says "Variedad eliminada" and carries no data. The row is gone. On a computer the next variety by name is selected, else the previous, else the empty state.
- **What can go wrong, and recovery:**
  - Cancel in the dialog leaves the person on the page with nothing changed.
  - A sale line or stock entry appeared after the page loaded. The delete is refused, the page refreshes, and it now shows "Archivar variedad" in place of delete. Nothing is lost, and the person continues in the archive flow.
  - A partner already deleted the variety. The person sees "Eso ya no está. La lista ya está al día." on the list, or "Eso ya no está." with "Volver a Variedades" on a detail.
  - After the delete, the variety's name is free at once, so the person can add it again.
- **Ends at:** Variedades, with the variety gone and its prices, threshold and attributes gone with it.
- **Touches:**
  - From the Variedades tab on a phone: 3 (row, "Eliminar variedad", confirm). From another tab it is 4, counting the tab.
  - On a computer: 2 if the variety is already selected (action, confirm), 3 if it needs selecting first.

### Flow: Archive a variety that has history

- **Starts at:** Variedades, or the variety's page.
- **Steps:**
  1. Open the variety's page, as in the delete flow.
  2. Tap "Archivar variedad". With history, this is the only removal action shown. **[no screen drawn]**
  3. A confirmation asks. If stock is not zero, it names the grams on hand, for example "Te quedan 12 g. Si la archivás, dejan de verse en Inicio y en las listas, pero el stock queda guardado." With zero stock, it only asks. The exact wording is design's call. Confirm.
  4. The person returns to Variedades. The toast says "Variedad archivada". The variety leaves the list and the summary line, and the selection moves as in delete. The "Archivadas (N)" link at the foot appears or its count goes up.
- **What can go wrong, and recovery:**
  - Cancel leaves everything as it was.
  - The variety is already gone: the "Eso ya no está" messages above.
  - The page showed delete but history has appeared since. See the stale-page recovery in the delete flow.
  - Nothing is lost if a partner archives it first. The person sees the same state.
- **Ends at:** Variedades. The variety no longer appears in Inicio's Lo que te queda, Nueva venta, the stock entry form, the price entry form, the catalog text or the pinned catalog. The pinned catalog is updated, and a hidden flag is kept. Past records still show the plain name.
- **Touches:** same as delete: 3 from the Variedades tab on a phone (4 from another tab), and 2–3 on a computer.

### Flow: Find archived varieties and restore one

- **Starts at:** Variedades, a tab root. The person looks for a variety that is no longer in the list.
- **Steps, by the card:**
  1. Tap "Archivadas (N)" at the foot of Variedades, next to "Todas las entradas". It is absent when N=0. **[no screen drawn]**
  2. The `/varieties/archived` card opens with "‹ Variedades". Each variety is listed by name, marked "Archivada" in words, with "Restaurar". On a computer it uses the same two panes and selects the first archived variety by name. **[no screen drawn]**
  3. Tap "Restaurar". There is no confirmation. The toast says "Variedad restaurada". The variety goes back to Variedades, Inicio, the pickers and the catalog. The pinned catalog is updated, and a hidden flag is kept.
- **Steps, by the page (to check before restoring):**
  1. On the Archivadas card, tap the row. This opens `/varieties/{low}`, read-only, with "‹ Archivadas". Its only action is "Restaurar". It has no "+ Entrada", "Nuevo precio", price delete, threshold switch, rename, Editar forms or removal action. **[no screen drawn]**
  2. Tap "Restaurar", with the same result.
- **What can go wrong, and recovery:**
  - The variety is already gone or already restored: "Eso ya no está. La lista ya está al día." The list refreshes.
  - Restaurar after another variety took the name: not possible, because an archived variety keeps its name reserved.
  - **Delete after history is lost:** if an archived variety's history has all been removed, there is no delete on the archived page. The person restores it first, then deletes it from its page. There is no second path.
  - The person tries to add or rename a variety to an archived name. It is refused inline: the name belongs to an archived variety, look in Archivadas. The person goes to Archivadas and can restore it.
- **Ends at:** Archivadas, with the count lowered. When it reaches 0 the link is gone from Variedades. The restored variety is back in Variedades.
- **Touches:**
  - From the Variedades tab: 2 (Archivadas (N), Restaurar). From another tab it is 3.
  - Via the page: 3 from Variedades.
  - Restore then delete: restore (2–3) plus delete (3–4).

### Flow: Recording something against a variety that was just archived

- **Starts at:** a form already open: Nueva venta, the stock entry form, the price entry form, or an edit form. A partner archives the variety meanwhile.
- **Steps:**
  1. The person saves.
  2. The save is refused with "Eso ya no está". Typed content is kept where the screen allows. In Nueva venta the line shows the generic gone message and the form stays open.
  3. The person removes or changes the line, or closes with "Cerrar" (it confirms if there are unsaved changes).
- **Recovery:** pick another variety from the picker, which no longer lists the archived one. Or restore it through Archivadas (flow above) and start again.
- **Ends at:** the same form, or the screen it was opened from.
- **Touches:** no extra touches in a normal run. In the refused case, 1–2 more to fix the line.

### Flow: Existing records of an archived variety

- **Starts at:** Ventas, sale detail, the customer account, Todas las entradas, Análisis, Ganancia and Caja, or the export.
- **Steps:** unchanged. The variety shows under its plain name, with no "archivada" tag. Entries can be edited or deleted. A sale that contains the variety can be deleted (Deshacer included), and Asignar cliente works.
- **What can go wrong, and recovery:** an entry edit cannot move onto a different archived variety, and the picker leaves archived varieties out. The person picks an unarchived one.
- **Ends at:** the same screens as before.
- **Touches:** unchanged.

### Flow: Jobs whose flow does not change

Archived varieties are only left out of lists and pickers. No step is added or removed.

- **Nueva venta:** unchanged. The picker has fewer rows.
- **Stock entry ("+ Entrada de stock"):** unchanged, within 2 taps. The picker has fewer rows.
- **Price entry:** unchanged. The form has fewer varieties.
- **Catalog and "Mandar catálogo":** unchanged, within 2 taps. The text and the "what does not go out" list leave archived varieties out. The pinned catalog updates by itself.
- **Inicio (Lo que te queda and "Arrancá por las variedades"):** unchanged. Archived varieties do not count.
- **Variedades summary line:** unchanged. It counts only unarchived varieties.
- **Navigation:** unchanged. Variedades stays a tab root, and Archivadas and the archived variety's page are cards with a back arrow and no "+ Nueva venta".

### Gaps for UX Lead

- No screen exists for the Archivadas card (phone or computer two-pane) or the archived variety's page.
- I did not verify whether `VariedadDetalle` has a place for "Eliminar variedad" and "Archivar variedad".
- No screen draws the destructive ConfirmDialog, the archive confirmation with the grams line, or the inline name-reserved message.
- The place of "Archivadas (N)" next to "Todas las entradas" is not drawn on `Variedades` or `VariedadesCompu`.
- The touch counts above assume these screens exist as the PRD describes them.

## UX Lead

_The system denied 1 write attempt: this role writes nothing; reproduce by reading the code, the task documents and the existing tests, or by running the declared checks. A denial here is not a syntax problem._

### Walk

I walked the prototype in `docs/product/design-system/pantallas` from the Variedades tab: `Variedades.dc.html` (phone), `VariedadesCompu.dc.html` (two panes), `VariedadDetalle.dc.html`, and `Estados.dc.html` for the confirmation pattern. No screen draws "Archivar", "Eliminar variedad", "Restaurar" or "Archivadas". The only "Eliminar" in the prototype is on ConfiguracionCategorias, for categories.

**Job 1: delete a variety that never moved**
- *Step 1, open the variety.* I walked it. On the phone, the Variedades row opens `VariedadDetalle` with "‹ Variedades". On the computer, `VariedadesCompu` shows Golden Cookies already selected in the right pane. This matches the flow.
- *Step 2, "Eliminar variedad".* **Sin prototipo.** Neither the phone page nor the computer pane draws a removal action. The page ends with "En el catálogo" and the footnote, and then the phone tab bar. The only trash control is the per-price "Borrar este precio". The flow should place the variety-level action so the two cannot be confused.
- *Step 3, destructive ConfirmDialog.* The pattern exists in Estados, "Antes de borrar" ("¿Borrás la venta?", "No, dejala" / "Sí, borrala"). The dialog to delete a variety reuses it. That example's text names a date and an amount, and the flow should say whether the variety dialog may name the variety.
- *Step 4, back to Variedades with toast "Variedad eliminada", next selection on the computer.* Sin prototipo for the toast and the reselection. The text is consistent with the PRD.
- *Recovery.* Cancel, the stale page that flips to "Archivar variedad", and "Eso ya no está" are all reachable and lose nothing. Judged on the text.

**Job 2: archive a variety that has history**
- *Steps 1–2.* The same as delete. "Archivar variedad" is **sin prototipo**.
- *Step 3, confirmation with the grams on hand.* **Sin prototipo.** It follows the Estados pattern, and the wording is design's.
- *Step 4, return to Variedades, "Archivadas (N)" appears.* The PRD only sends delete back to Variedades. The flow chooses this landing for archive too, which is reasonable, and should say it is a decision. The foot of both list screens draws only "Todas las entradas". Where "Archivadas (N)" goes next to it is **sin prototipo**.

**Job 3: find archived varieties and restore one**
- *Card path: "Archivadas (N)" → Archivadas → "Restaurar".* **Sin prototipo** for all of it. On the computer, the flow does not say where "Restaurar" sits across the two panes. The job completes in 2 touches as written.
- *Page path: archived `VariedadDetalle`, read-only.* **Sin prototipo.** The live page shows that catalog attributes are edited **inline** (Emoji, % índica, Aroma/sabor inputs and the "Mostrar en el catálogo" switch), not through an "Editar" form. The flow's list of removed controls does not name them. The page also links "Ver todas" to TodasLasEntradas and to VerTodas, which has its own price delete, and the flow does not say what happens to those links on an archived page. Nor does it say where the person lands after restoring from the page.
- *Recovery.* Already gone or already restored: the list refreshes. An archived variety that has lost its history: restore, then delete. Both paths work.
- *Inline refusal for a reserved name on VariedadNueva.* **Sin prototipo.** The recovery means leaving the form for Archivadas, and the flow does not say whether a link is offered or whether the typed input survives.

**Job 4: a form left open while a partner archives the variety**
- Nueva venta, EntradaStock and PrecioNuevo exist. The refused-on-save state is **sin prototipo** for this case. It reuses sales.md's existing "not found" line behaviour, and the person can pick another variety or close. The job completes.

**Job 5: existing records of an archived variety**
- Unchanged screens: Ventas, VentaDetalle, ClienteFicha, TodasLasEntradas, Análisis. The plain name shows with no tag, so nothing new needs drawing. Inicio, Catalogo and EntradaStock link to `VariedadDetalle`, but archived varieties leave those lists, so the only way into an archived page is through Archivadas. That is consistent with "‹ Archivadas".

**Jobs whose flow does not change**
- I walked Nueva venta, EntradaStock, PrecioNuevo, Catalogo and Inicio. Their pickers and lists only get shorter, so no step changes.

No step is a dead end, and every failure has a recovery. Every gap above is a screen not drawn yet or a detail for screens design.

### Verdict

**approved**: Every job can be completed, and every failure has a way back. What's left are new screens with nothing drawn yet ("sin prototipo") and a few points to tighten for screens design. None of them stops a job.

## UI Designer

### Screens

Source: `docs/product/design-system/pantallas/` (Variedades, VariedadesCompu, VariedadDetalle, VariedadNueva, Estados, Catalogo, Inicio, NuevaVenta, EntradaStock, PrecioNuevo). I read it through UX Lead's walk. No screen draws "Archivar variedad", "Eliminar variedad", "Restaurar", the Archivadas card, or an archived variety's page. For each, I say which existing components build it and list the gap for UI Lead. I add no new visual element, token or chip style.

### Variedades (phone) — `Variedades`, tab root

- **Built from:** the existing screen as is (bar, "+ Nueva venta", summary line, "Mandar catálogo", "+ Entrada de stock", "Nueva variedad", rows with `MarcaEstado`, "Oculta", grams, days left, price, foot link "Todas las entradas").
- **States:**
  - filled: unarchived varieties only, by name;
  - empty (no varieties, none archived): the existing empty state, no summary line;
  - loading and error: the shared `Estados` loading and retry;
  - offline: "Sin conexión";
  - the summary line counts unarchived varieties only (all ok, or the mix by word).
- **PRD change:**
  - Archived varieties are left out of the list and the summary line.
  - Add "Archivadas (N)" at the foot, next to "Todas las entradas". It uses the same text-link treatment as "Todas las entradas". It is absent when N = 0.
  - Toasts arriving here after removal use the existing `Estados` toast: "Variedad eliminada", "Variedad archivada". They carry no data.
  - After a stale or already-gone removal, show the existing notice "Eso ya no está. La lista ya está al día."
- **Gap:** the placement of "Archivadas (N)" beside "Todas las entradas" is not drawn. I compose it from that link's style, as two links on the foot line.

### VariedadesCompu — two panes, tab root

- **Built from:** the existing screen as is.
- **States:**
  - filled: the first unarchived variety by name is selected on open;
  - after archiving or deleting the selected one: the next by name, else the previous, else the empty state;
  - empty: no variety: the right pane holds the existing empty treatment, with no figure;
  - loading, error and offline: as above.
- **PRD change:** the same as the phone: archived out of the list and summary, plus the "Archivadas (N)" foot link. The removal action sits in the right pane, which is the live `VariedadDetalle` content.

### VariedadDetalle (live variety) — card, "‹ Variedades"

- **Built from:** the existing screen, unchanged in structure (summary line, "+ Entrada" and "Nuevo precio", Stock y entradas, Precios, Aviso de stock bajo, En el catálogo, footnote).
- **States:**
  - filled, no history: shows "Eliminar variedad";
  - filled, with history: shows "Archivar variedad";
  - loading, error and offline: shared `Estados`;
  - the variety is gone: "Eso ya no está." with "Volver a Variedades";
  - stale: delete was shown but history appeared; the delete is refused with its coded refusal, the page refreshes in place and now shows "Archivar variedad". No dead end.
- **PRD change:**
  - One removal action at the foot of the page, after "En el catálogo" and the footnote. It is built from the existing destructive text-button treatment, the same one the page's "Borrar este precio" uses and the one ConfiguracionCategorias uses for "Eliminar". It is set apart from the per-price delete by its position at the end of the page. It reads "Eliminar variedad" or "Archivar variedad", never both.
  - "Archivar variedad" is a calmer action than delete. If UI Lead finds the destructive style wrong for it, use the neutral secondary button style.
  - On the computer, the same action sits at the foot of the right pane.
- **Gap:** not drawn. Also, the live page draws catalog attributes inline, while `varieties.md` says "Editar" forms (an agreed departure listed for the PR). I am not redrawing that. UI Lead should confirm which applies.

### Confirmation dialogs — `Estados` "Antes de borrar" pattern

- **Built from:** the existing ConfirmDialog (title, body, two buttons; "No, dejala" and "Sí, borrala").
- **Delete variety:**
  - body: asks to delete this variety for good, and says its prices, threshold and catalog attributes go with it;
  - the destructive confirm button reads "Sí, borrala" (the dialog's existing pair);
  - the title may name the variety (a name is not personal data).
- **Archive variety:**
  - with stock not zero: names the grams on hand in the app's grams format, for example "Te quedan 12 g. Si la archivás, dejan de verse en Inicio y en las listas, pero el stock queda guardado.";
  - with zero stock: a plain question with no grams line;
  - buttons: a neutral cancel and "Archivá", wording is design's.
  - It uses the same dialog frame, with no destructive emphasis.
- **States:** open, working (buttons disabled), and failure. A refusal closes the dialog and refreshes the page. A gone variety closes it and shows the "Eso ya no está" notice.
- **Gap:** neither dialog is drawn as such. Only the pattern exists.

### Archivadas card — new, `/varieties/archived`, "‹ Variedades"

- **Built from:** the `Variedades` list screen: card chrome (bar or sidebar, "‹ Variedades", no "+ Nueva venta"), the same row component for each variety.
- **Row:** the name, the word "Archivada" in the place the status word sits (a word with the existing chip shape), and a "Restaurar" button on the right built from the secondary small button of other rows ("Cobrar" in Clientes is the model). The rest of the row opens the archived variety's page. Do not draw the status mark as live.
- **States:**
  - filled;
  - empty: not reachable from the foot link. If reached by a direct link or after restoring the last one, show a fixed line using the existing empty-state treatment, for example "No tenés variedades archivadas";
  - loading, error and offline: shared `Estados`;
  - after "Restaurar": the toast "Variedad restaurada", and the row leaves the list. The count in Variedades updates;
  - restoring one already gone: "Eso ya no está. La lista ya está al día."
- **Computer:** the same two panes as `VariedadesCompu`. The left pane lists archived varieties and the right pane shows the selected archived variety's page. The first archived variety is selected on open. "Restaurar" is on the row and on the right pane.
- **Gap:** not drawn on phone or computer.

### Archived variety's page — new, `/varieties/{low}` read-only, "‹ Archivadas"

- **Built from:** `VariedadDetalle`, read-only.
- **Shows:**
  - the summary line, with "Archivada" in words (the same chip as the Archivadas row) and "Oculta" if the flag is set;
  - the sections Stock y entradas, Precios, Aviso de stock bajo and En el catálogo as plain read-only text.
- **Removes:**
  - "+ Entrada" and "Nuevo precio";
  - per-price delete;
  - the threshold switch and its "Editar";
  - every attribute input and switch, which show as plain read-only values;
  - rename;
  - the removal action.
- **Single action:** "Restaurar", in the place the live page's two buttons occupy (or the removal action's foot, UI Lead's call). One tap, no confirmation. After it: the toast "Variedad restaurada" and back to the list it came from (Archivadas); the page does not stay on screen as a live one.
- **States:** filled; loading, error and offline (shared); gone: "Eso ya no está." with "Volver a Variedades". A variety already restored by a partner opens as live (no longer archived), and the page refreshes.
- **"Ver todas" links:** they keep opening the read-only list of entries and prices. On those lists, the per-entry edit and delete stay available for entries, as the PRD keeps existing records workable (Todas las entradas allows edit and delete). The per-price delete is not offered for an archived variety.
- **Gap:** not drawn. Where UX Lead asked about the "Ver todas" links, I propose keeping them as read lists with entry actions as above; UI Lead should confirm.

### VariedadNueva and the rename form — form, "Cerrar"

- **Built from:** the existing form, unchanged.
- **PRD change:** a new inline refusal under the name field when the name belongs to an archived variety. It uses the existing field-error treatment (the same as the duplicate name refusal), with a fixed line such as "Ese nombre es de una variedad archivada. Fijate en Archivadas." It repeats nothing typed and the typed text is kept.
- **Gap:** the refusal is not drawn. It offers no link, only the sentence. A link to Archivadas would need a form-leaving confirmation (unsaved changes), which I do not draw. UI Lead decides.

### Nueva venta, EntradaStock, PrecioNuevo — form or sheet pickers

- **Built from:** the existing screens as is.
- **PRD change:** archived varieties are not offered in the variety pickers. This changes only the rows.
- **New state (form left open while a partner archives):**
  - Nueva venta: the line shows the existing generic gone message for a variety not found and the form stays open with everything typed;
  - EntradaStock and PrecioNuevo: the save closes nothing, keeps the rest of what was typed, and shows "Eso ya no está. La lista ya está al día." per the existing gone rule;
  - editing an existing entry keeps its archived variety, which shows as a plain name, and does not offer other archived ones.

### Catalogo — card, "‹ Variedades"

- **Built from:** the existing screen as is.
- **PRD change:** archived varieties are absent from the preview and from "What does not go out". No reason chip for them. Reasons stay hidden, no price today, sold out or no entries. States unchanged.

### Inicio (phone, `InicioOscuro`, `InicioCompu`)

- **Built from:** the existing screens as is.
- **PRD change:** "Lo que te queda" leaves out archived varieties. "Arrancá por las variedades" does not count them. All period figures stay the same, and nothing else on Inicio changes. States unchanged.

### Past-record screens — Ventas, VentaDetalle, ClienteFicha, TodasLasEntradas, Análisis

- **Built from:** the existing screens as is.
- **PRD change:** none visually. An archived variety shows by its plain name, with no "archivada" tag, and every figure is unchanged. In Todas las entradas, entries of archived varieties keep their edit and delete.

### Estados

- **Built from:** the existing toast, notice, ConfirmDialog, loading, retry and gone states.
- **PRD change:** new fixed toast texts only: "Variedad eliminada", "Variedad archivada", "Variedad restaurada". They carry no data and no "Deshacer". Both themes follow tokens. No state is told by color alone: "Archivada" is a word in a chip, the removal action is a labelled button.

### Gaps for UI Lead to flag (no drawing for any of these)

- Archivadas card, phone and computer.
- The archived variety's page.
- The removal action's placement on `VariedadDetalle` and its pane on `VariedadesCompu`.
- The delete and archive dialogs, including the grams line.
- "Archivadas (N)" on the foot of both list screens.
- The reserved-name refusal on `VariedadNueva` and the rename form.
- Whether the live page's inline catalog attribute controls match the "Editar" form in `varieties.md`.
- The three new toasts and the empty line for Archivadas.
