#### Archive and restore: domain read-only rule, routes, archived list, name_archived

Builds on A1. Files: `app/varieties/domain/variety.py`, `application/manage_varieties.py`, `application/manage_prices.py`, `application/errors.py`, `adapters/router.py`, `adapters/schemas.py`, `adapters/sql_varieties.py`, `contract.json` and `refusals.ts`.

**Domain:**
- `Variety.archive(at)` sets `archived_at` only when it is `None` (idempotent).
- `Variety.restore()` clears it (idempotent).
- An archived `Variety` refuses `rename`, recording a price entry and deleting a price entry with `VarietyArchived` (`variety.archived`, 404 gone). That makes archived varieties read-only in the domain itself.

**Use cases:** `archive_variety` and `restore_variety`.
- `get` → missing answers `variety.not_found`.
- Then call the domain method, `save` (which persists `archived_at`) and commit once.
- The instant comes from the injected clock (`app/clock.py`), never from the request.
- **Archive is never refused because the variety has or lacks history** (PM).
- Restore changes nothing else: prices, entries, threshold, catalog attributes and the hidden flag were never touched.

**Names:** `register_variety` and `rename_variety` raise the new `NameHeldByArchived`, code `variety.name_archived` (409, kind `refusal`), when `name_holder` answers `ARCHIVED`. It has fixed text and interpolates nothing. `LIVE` keeps `NameTaken`.
- Add the code to `contract.json`.
- Add its `refusals.ts` entry: field name, with text like "Ese nombre es de una variedad archivada. Buscala en Archivadas."

**Routes:**
- `POST /varieties/{id}/archive` and `POST /varieties/{id}/restore` answer 200 with the variety.
- `GET /varieties?archived=true` answers `archived()`.
- `VarietyOut` (list and detail) gains `archived: bool`.
- The router is already mounted with `upkeep_gated`, so both writes trigger upkeep after commit (ADR-0014). Add no new wiring.

**Tests, HTTP first:**
- the archive and restore columns of the removal matrix (live with and without history, archived, missing and another business's);
- archive is idempotent and restore is idempotent;
- a renamed or price-added archived variety answers `variety.archived`;
- an archived name in any case or spacing is refused on add and on rename with `variety.name_archived`;
- `?archived=true` lists only archived varieties, by name;
- both new routes join `tests/test_business_isolation.py` (seeder plus `BODIES`) and `tests/test_gated_routes.py`. Another business's variety, archived or not, answers 404.

Seams: ``Variety` domain unit tests (archive, restore, read-only rule)`, `HTTP `POST /varieties/{id}/archive`, `POST /varieties/{id}/restore`, `GET /varieties?archived=true`, `POST /varieties`, `PATCH /varieties/{id}`, `POST /varieties/{id}/prices``.

Depends on: nothing.

#### Stale references refused as gone in sales, stock and catalog; archived stock rows; per-reader hiding tests

Builds on A1. It touches only sales', stock's and catalog's modules, never `app/varieties/`.

**Sales:** `SqlPriceBook.for_variety` (`app/sales/adapters/price_book.py`) answers `None` for an archived variety, as for a missing one. `record_sale` then raises `UnknownVariety`, so the answer is `sale.variety_not_found` and Nueva venta's existing dispatch is untouched. `PlannedPriceBook` is unchanged.

**Stock:**
- `VarietyCatalog` (`app/stock/application/ports.py`) gains `usable(variety_id) -> bool` beside `exists`. `SqlVarietyCatalog` implements it from `get(id)` and `is_archived`.
- **Recording an entry** requires `usable`.
- **Editing an entry** requires `exists` when its `variety_id` is unchanged (it may keep its own archived variety), and `usable` when the variety changes.
- **Setting or turning off a threshold** requires `usable`.
- Missing still raises stock's internal `VarietyNotFound`. Archived raises a new internal, code-less `VarietyArchived` signal in stock's `application/errors.py`. The stock router re-raises it as varieties' `VarietyArchived` (`variety.archived`), the same pattern it already uses for not-found.
- `GET /stock?archived=true` answers the archived varieties' rows: the same `stock_level` over the same load, filtered to `archived()`. It is used by the archived page.

**Catalog:**
- `CatalogVarieties.exists` becomes `usable`, so editing catalog attributes of an archived variety is refused.
- The catalog router answers it as varieties' `VarietyArchived` (`variety.archived`). Missing keeps `catalog.variety_not_found`.
- The text and the "does not go out" list already come from `listed()` (A1).

**Tests:**
- A small test helper under `tests/fakes/` sets `archived_at` on a variety row directly, since there is no archive route on this branch.
- HTTP tests, each in its owner's `tests/<capability>/`, show an archived variety:
  - absent from `GET /catalog` (text and "does not go out");
  - absent from `GET /panel` stock rows and `GET /stock` rows;
  - present in `GET /stock?archived=true`.
- `GET /stock`'s `total` is unchanged by archiving.
- `POST /sales` with an archived variety answers 404 `sale.variety_not_found`, keeps 0 rows written, and leaves the form-side contract unchanged.
- `POST /stock/entries` and the threshold route answer 404 `variety.archived`.
- Editing an entry that keeps its archived variety succeeds. Moving an entry onto another archived variety answers `variety.archived`.
- Deleting such an entry, deleting a sale containing an archived variety, and assigning a customer to it all still work.
- `PUT /catalog/varieties/{id}` on an archived variety answers `variety.archived`.
- The sweep's existing assertions still pass. Add `?archived=true` on `/stock` to its list checks.

Seams: `HTTP `GET /stock` and `GET /stock?archived=true`, `POST /stock/entries`, the entry edit and delete routes, the threshold route`, `HTTP `POST /sales`, `DELETE /sales/{id}`, assign customer`, `HTTP `GET /catalog`, `PUT /catalog/varieties/{id}``, `HTTP `GET /panel``.

Depends on: `a1-flag-and-readers`.

#### Delete a variety with no history: guarded delete, dependents, offered action, foreign-key race

Builds on A2 and C. It shares varieties' files with A2 and stock's `manage_entries` with C.

**Repository (`SqlVarieties`):**
- One private predicate `_history_exists(variety_id)`: two `EXISTS` subqueries over `SaleLineRow` and `StockEntryRow`, each filtered on `variety_id` and on `business_id`. The adapter imports those tables; adapters may cross.
- Port methods:
  - `has_history(variety_id) -> bool`;
  - `remove_price_entries(variety_id)`, a Core `delete()`;
  - `remove_unused(variety_id) -> bool`, which runs `DELETE FROM variety WHERE id=:id AND business_id=:b AND NOT (<history>) RETURNING id`.
- Use Core statements only, never mutate `row.price_entries`.

**Dependents:** varieties declares `VarietyLeaving(Protocol): def leaving(self, variety_id: int) -> None` in `application/ports.py`.
- Stock implements it in its adapters by deleting that variety's `stock_threshold` row.
- Catalog implements it by deleting that variety's `catalog_attributes` row.
- `app/main.py` composes both as one `(Session, business_id)` factory on `app.state` (ADR-0006's composite). The varieties router reads it the way other factories are read.

**Use case `delete_variety`, in order:**
1. `get`: missing answers `variety.not_found`; archived answers `variety.archived` (restore first).
2. `has_history`: true answers `VarietyHasHistory`, code `variety.has_history` (409 refusal), with nothing written.
3. `leaving.leaving(id)`, then `remove_price_entries`.
4. `remove_unused`: if no row comes back, `transaction.rollback()` and the same refusal.
5. One commit.

Add the code to `contract.json` and `refusals.ts`.

**Routes:**
- `DELETE /varieties/{id}` answers 204.
- `GET /varieties/{id}` gains `removal: "delete" | "archive" | null`: `null` when archived, otherwise from `has_history`. The list does not carry it.

**Foreign-key race:** the repositories that insert a reference to a variety wrap their flush. Those are sales' record path (`sql_sales.py`), stock's entry add and save (`sql_stock_entries.py`) and varieties' price entry save.
- Catch `IntegrityError` only to set a flag.
- Outside the `except`, raise an internal, code-less `ReferenceRefused` (ADR-0005: nothing of the driver's message stays reachable).
- `record_sale`, `record_entry`, `edit_entry` and `record_price` catch it, call `transaction.rollback()`, and re-ask their own lookup (`price_book.for_variety`, `catalog.usable` or `exists`, `get`).
  - If the variety is gone or archived, raise their gone code: `sale.variety_not_found`, `variety.not_found` or `variety.archived`.
  - Otherwise re-raise the original error, so another foreign-key bug stays a 500.

**Tests:**
- **Offered-action and delete matrix over HTTP:** history from each source alone (a list line, a gift, a special price, an entry of each origin, both adjustment signs). Price entries, a threshold and attributes alone leave `delete`. Deleting the last sale or entry offers `delete` again. An archived variety whose history went away, once restored, offers `delete`.
- A deleted variety takes its price entries, threshold and attributes with it, and its name is accepted again at once.
- **Use-case tests for both re-ask branches:** a fake whose flush raises the signal, with the lookup saying gone (gone code) and with the lookup saying present (re-raised).
- **Repository tests over a real SQLite file, both `sqlite:///` and `sqlite+libsql:///`:**
  - `remove_unused` returns the id with no history, and nothing with history;
  - after a forced rollback following the dependent deletes, thresholds, attributes and price entries are all still there;
  - a two-connection race in both orders: delete first means the insert is refused as gone; insert first means the delete answers `variety.has_history`. 0 rows reference a missing variety.
- `DELETE /varieties/{id}` joins the isolation sweep and the gated-route test.

Seams: `HTTP `DELETE /varieties/{id}` and `GET /varieties/{id}``, `use cases `delete_variety`, `record_sale`, `record_entry`, `edit_entry`, `record_price` with a fake repository raising `ReferenceRefused``, ``SqlVarieties` repository over real SQLite files (sqlite and libsql), two connections`.

Depends on: `a2-archive-restore`, `c-readers-and-pickers`.

#### The books don't move: before/after-archive/after-restore capture, comes-back-whole, and upkeep on archive and restore

Builds on A2 and C, branched from master after both merged. Test code only. If a figure moves, the fix lands here with the test that found it.

**`tests/varieties/test_archive_moves_no_figure.py`**, written first, at the HTTP seam with the `client` fixture and the movable clock.

Seed, through the API:
- two varieties, one to archive;
- a purchase;
- sales left owing and paid on the spot, a gift and a special price;
- a later payment;
- a negative adjustment with costed grams and one with pending grams;
- a sale line with pending grams;
- an expense.

Capture three times (before archiving, after `POST /varieties/{id}/archive`, after `POST /varieties/{id}/restore`) and assert 0 differences:
- `GET /analysis?month=` for every month of the history, with its weeks, months, weekdays and all-time window;
- `GET /panel`'s period figures (today, week, month);
- every `GET /sales/{id}`, lines and costs included;
- every customer's balance and account;
- `GET /stock`'s `total`;
- the archived variety's stock, status and pending grams. Read these from `GET /stock` before archiving and after restoring, and from `GET /stock?archived=true` while archived.

**Comes back whole:** after restoring, the variety's prices, entries, stock, threshold, catalog attributes and hidden flag equal what they were before. Run it once with the variety hidden and once shown, and confirm it is back in `GET /varieties`, `GET /catalog` and the panel's stock rows.

**Upkeep:** using the existing fake Telegram with a published pin, archive and restore each attempt an edit exactly when the built text differs from the last published text. A hidden or unpriced variety's archive sends nothing. The triggering request answers normally even when the fake fails.

Seams: `HTTP `POST /varieties/{id}/archive` and `/restore`, `GET /analysis`, `GET /panel`, `GET /sales/{id}`, customer balance and account routes, `GET /stock`, `GET /catalog``, `the existing fake Telegram adapter`.

#### Front: the removal action on a variety's page, its confirmations, refusals and two-pane reselection

Builds on B and E. Work in `web/src/` over `GET /varieties/{id}`'s `archived` and `removal`, using the api layer and its types, `VariedadDetalle`, the confirmation copy and the Variedades two-pane container.

**API types:** add `archived: boolean` and `removal: 'delete' | 'archive' | null` to the variety type. Add client calls for archive, restore and delete.

**On a live variety's page, exactly one removal action, from `removal`:**
- **`delete`:** "Eliminar variedad" opens a destructive ConfirmDialog. Then the toast "Variedad eliminada" and back to Variedades.
- **`archive`:** "Archivar variedad" opens a confirmation.
  - With stock not zero, it names the grams on hand from the stock row, formatted `12 g`: "Te quedan 12 g. Si la archivás, dejan de verse en Inicio y en las listas, pero el stock queda guardado."
  - With zero stock it simply asks. The exact wording follows the design.
  - Then the toast "Variedad archivada" and back to Variedades.
  - Toasts carry no name or amount.
- **`variety.has_history` on delete (stale page):** refetch the page, which then shows "Archivar variedad". No toast.
- **Archive never meets a history refusal.**
- **A gone answer** (`variety.not_found` or `variety.archived`) on confirm closes the dialog, refreshes the list and shows "Eso ya no está. La lista ya está al día."

**Name refusal:** `variety.name_archived` shows inline on the name field in the add and rename forms, keeping what was typed.

**Stock entry edit form:** editing an entry whose variety is archived shows that variety, by the entry's own name, as the chosen one. The picker otherwise offers only `GET /varieties` (listed) varieties.

**Computer:** archiving or deleting the selected variety selects the next one by name, else the previous one, else the empty state. First selection needs no change, since the list is unarchived only.

- the gone notice;
- `name_archived` inline;
- the computer reselection;
- no name or amount in toasts, the console or storage.

Run the design comparison of the variety page (phone and computer, both themes) and attach it.

### Changes
- `app/migrations/versions/0012_archived_variety.py`: native `ADD COLUMN archived_at` (UtcDateTime, nullable, no default, no backfill). The downgrade raises a fixed message naming no data while any variety is archived, then runs a native `DROP COLUMN`.
- `app/varieties/adapters/tables.py`: `VarietyRow.archived_at`.
- `app/varieties/domain/variety.py`: `Variety.archived_at`, `is_archived`, and the `NameHolder` enum (`LIVE`, `ARCHIVED`).
- `app/varieties/application/ports.py` and `adapters/sql_varieties.py`: `all()` removed; `listed()` and `archived()` added, both ordered by `name_key`; `get` answers any variety of the business; `name_taken` replaced by `name_holder(name, other_than=)`.
- `app/varieties/application/errors.py` and `adapters/router.py`: `VarietyArchived`, code `variety.archived`, registered under 404.
- `app/varieties/application/manage_varieties.py`: register and rename use `name_holder(...) is not None`; `list_varieties` uses `listed()`.
- `app/catalog/adapters/sql_catalog_varieties.py` and `app/stock/adapters/stock_figures.py`: use `listed()`.
- `app/stock/adapters/variety_catalog.py`, `app/stock/application/ports.py` and `read_stock.py`: `ids()` became `listed_ids()`. `read_stock` computes each variety's level once. The total is summed over the unfiltered `grouped` load, and the rows are the listed varieties only.
- `web/src/api/contract.json`: `variety.archived` as `gone`. `web/src/api/refusals.test.ts`: `isGone` assertion for it.
- `docs/adr/0031-a-variety-is-archived-by-a-stored-instant-and-its-history-is-computed.md`.
- `web/e2e/ventasPhone.spec.ts` (outside the subtask, see below): the spec's "today" is now taken in the business timezone.

### Tests
- `tests/migrations/test_0012_archived_variety.py`, over a sqlite file and a libsql file. It upgrades to 0011 and seeds two businesses with a row in each of the five referencing tables. It then upgrades to head with `upgrade_to_head` and sets one `archived_at`. The downgrade must refuse with a message that names no data. After clearing the flag the downgrade runs, row counts are unchanged, `PRAGMA foreign_key_check` is empty, and a second upgrade works. This is the rejecting input for the downgrade guard.
- `tests/varieties/test_variety.py`: a variety is live until it carries `archived_at`.
- `tests/varieties/test_varieties_api.py`: with `archived_at` set by hand, the variety is absent from `GET /varieties` but still returned by `GET /varieties/{id}`. Its name still blocks add and rename with 409.
- `tests/stock/test_read_stock_api.py`: an archived variety leaves the rows of `GET /stock` but its grams stay in `total`.
- `tests/business_removal/test_migrated_schema_types.py`: `variety.archived_at` is in the migrated schema, is not secret-like, and is not an unwritable type, so the export picks it up unchanged.
- The existing `tests/migrations/test_naming.py` passes with no allowlist change.

### Decisions and assumptions
- `NameHolder` is in the domain module; the brief allowed domain or application.
- `refusals.ts` has no per-gone-code table (dispatch is by kind from `contract.json`), so `contract.json` is the only registration; the `refusals.test.ts` assertion checks it.
- `has_records` already counts the whole `variety` table directly and never used `all()`, so it is unchanged.
- `SqlVarieties.save` does not touch `archived_at`; archive and restore are A2's.
- I ran `ruff format` only on files I created or edited. The repo has 75 other files that already fail `ruff format --check`, and the declared checks do not include it.
- `web/e2e/ventasPhone.spec.ts` is outside the subtask. The delivery gate refused on it: the spec took today's date in UTC and posted a sale, and the server answered `422 sale.date_in_future` whenever the Buenos Aires date was behind UTC. I changed it to take the date in `America/Argentina/Buenos_Aires`, matching `app/clock.py`, and committed that on its own. It is a flake that depends on the hour of the run, not related to this feature.

### For the Tech Lead
- Look first at `read_stock.py`. It now computes `levels` for every key in `grouped` and sums those for `total`. Rows come from `listed_ids()`, and a listed variety with no entries gets a zero level computed on the spot.
- Check the 0012 downgrade message in the migration file; it says nothing about the data.
- The ADR also records the later subtasks' decisions (guarded delete, rollback-and-reask, accepted race) in the brief's wording; it does not implement them.
- The full pytest run has no failures, and mypy, ruff, web typecheck and web lint are clean. I ran the web unit tests for `src/api` only. After the e2e fix I re-ran only `ventasPhone.spec.ts` (it passes), not the whole e2e suite; the other 33 e2e tests had passed before it.

### Verdict

**approved**: Ran the seam tests (84 passed, libsql case included). Checked by reading: all() is removed, every caller uses listed(), the stock total comes from the unfiltered load, the migration uses native add/drop with a guarded downgrade, the §11 name is correct, and variety.archived is registered once.

### Findings

| severity | category | location | claim | evidence |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| observation | spec-scope | web/e2e/ventasPhone.spec.ts:62 | The diff changes an e2e spec outside the brief. The change is justified: the spec took "today" in UTC, while the server uses Buenos Aires time. It is committed on its own (fb46393). | The Developer's `### Decisions and assumptions` explains it; `web-e2e` passed on fb46393. |
| observation | standard-other | app/varieties/application/errors.py:24 | `VarietyArchived` puts the variety id in its message, as `VarietyNotFound` already does. It includes no name and no amount, so invariant 6 and §9 hold. | Read the file: `f"variety {variety_id} is archived"` |

What I verified by running or reading:
- **Seam tests.** `uv run --no-project pytest -n auto` on tests/migrations/test_0012_archived_variety.py, test_naming.py, tests/stock/test_read_stock_api.py, tests/varieties/test_varieties_api.py, test_variety.py and tests/business_removal/test_migrated_schema_types.py: `84 passed`, none skipped, so the libsql case ran.
- **Invariant 1.** `all()` is gone from the port and from `SqlVarieties`, and no caller of `varieties.all` or `catalog.ids` is left (grep). The direct `VarietyRow` joins left are in the sale views and the stock draws, and they must stay unfiltered.
- **Invariant 2.** `read_stock`'s `total` is summed over every key of the unfiltered `grouped` load; only the rows come from `listed_ids()`. The test (30 g + 20 g archived → total 50, one row) would fail under the old shared filter. Panel period figures, sale views and the export are not touched.
- **Invariant 6.** `variety.archived` appears once in `contract.json`, as `gone`. `refusals.ts` dispatches by kind from the contract, so it needs no table entry of its own, and `refusals.test.ts` asserts `isGone`.
- **Invariant 8.** The migration uses a native `add_column` and a native `DROP COLUMN`, with no rebuild. The downgrade guard is an enforcement artefact and sits at the declared Alembic seam. It is exercised on the input it must reject (one archived row → RuntimeError, with a message naming no data), over both drivers, with rows in all five referencing tables, and `foreign_key_check` is empty afterwards.
- **§11 naming.** `archived_at` is a nullable `UtcDateTime`, and `_at` names an instant, as the rule asks. `test_naming.py` passes, and its allowlist is unchanged in the diff.
- **Invariants 3, 4, 5 and 7.** The diff adds no route, no delete and no archive, so the Developer's "Not touched" claim for them is accurate.

### Changes
- `app/varieties/domain/variety.py`: `archived_at`, `is_archived`, idempotent `archive(at)` and `restore()`, `VarietyArchived` (`variety.archived`); an archived variety refuses `rename`, `record_price` and `delete_price`.
- `app/varieties/application`: `archive_variety` and `restore_variety` (get, domain method, save, commit once, never refused for history). `NameHeldByArchived` (`variety.name_archived`, fixed text) is raised from `register_variety` and `rename_variety` when `name_holder` answers `ARCHIVED`. `NameHolder` and `listed()`/`archived()`/`name_holder` replace `all()`/`name_taken` in `ports.py`.
- `app/varieties/adapters`: `archived_at` on `VarietyRow` (`UtcDateTime`) and persisted in `save`; `POST /varieties/{id}/archive` and `/restore`; `GET /varieties?archived=true`; `archived: bool` on `VarietyOut`; the new classes are registered in `as_http`.
- `app/migrations/versions/0012_variety_archived_at.py`: native ADD and DROP COLUMN.
- The three `.all()` callers (stock figures, stock `variety_catalog`, catalog varieties) now use `listed()`, or listed plus archived for `ids()`.
- `web/src/api/contract.json` gets `variety.archived` and `variety.name_archived`. `refusals.ts` exports `NAME_ARCHIVED_MESSAGE`, wired into the `VarietyNew` and `RenameForm` override tables.
- `web/e2e/ventasPhone.spec.ts`: the sale's date is now the Argentina day instead of the browser's UTC day.

### Tests
- Domain unit tests (`tests/varieties/test_variety.py`): archive sets the instant once, restore is idempotent, an archived variety refuses rename, record price and delete price, and a restored one renames again.
- HTTP (`tests/varieties/test_archive_api.py`):
  - the archive and restore columns of the removal matrix, with and without history;
  - idempotency, and a missing variety answers `variety.not_found`;
  - prices survive archive and restore;
  - archived rename and prices answer `variety.archived`;
  - an archived name in three casings and spacings is refused on add, and on rename, with `variety.name_archived`;
  - a live name still answers `variety.name_taken`;
  - the `?archived=true` list is by name.
- `tests/test_business_isolation.py`: another business reading, archiving, restoring, renaming or pricing an archived variety gets 404 `variety.not_found`. The two new routes are covered by the existing generic sweep and by `tests/test_gated_routes.py`.

### Decisions and assumptions
See the assumptions field. The main one: A1 was not on this branch, so I supplied its minimum (column, migration, readers, `name_holder`) and registered `variety.archived`, which will overlap A1 on merge.

### For the Tech Lead
- Look first at the overlap with A1: `tables.py`, `ports.py`, `sql_varieties.py`, migration 0012 and the `variety.archived` entry in `contract.json`. The same code or contract entry may arrive twice.
- Invariant 8 is only partly met: migration 0012 uses native ADD and DROP COLUMN, but its upgrade and downgrade test on the sqlite and libsql file drivers, with rows in the five referencing tables, is not written. It belongs to A1.
- `web/e2e/ventasPhone.spec.ts` is outside this subtask. Its "today" came from the browser's UTC day, so the e2e check failed from 21:00 Argentina time. I fixed that one spec, and it passes on its own.
- The `ruff format --check` has many pre-existing diffs (74 files) and is not a declared check, so I did not reformat.

Checked and found to hold:
- **Domain rules:** archive keeps its first instant and restore is idempotent. An archived variety refuses rename, recording a price and deleting a price with `variety.archived` (404).
- **Archive and restore:** neither is refused because of history (invariant 3). The archive instant comes from the injected clock.
- **Name refusal:** `variety.name_archived` has fixed text and is added once, with its `refusals.ts` entry.
- **Archived list:** `?archived=true` lists only archived varieties, by name.
- **Readers (invariant 1):** `all()` is gone; every caller now uses `listed()` or `archived()`.
- **Stock total (invariant 2):** `GET /stock`'s `total` still counts archived varieties, because `ids()` returns listed plus archived over the unfiltered `for_varieties(None)` load.
- **Isolation and gating (invariant 7):** both new routes are picked up by the generic isolation sweep and the gated-route test, which are green. A dedicated test also covers an archived variety of another business.
- **Naming (§11):** `archived_at` is a datetime column ending in `_at`, and `test_naming` passes.
- **Tests:** `tests/varieties/test_archive_api.py` and `tests/varieties/test_variety.py` give `44 passed`.

### Checks

| `lint` | `uv run --no-project ruff check .` | passed |
| `types` | `uv run --no-project mypy app tests` | passed |
| `web-types` | `npm --prefix web run typecheck` | passed |

| `web-e2e` | `npm --prefix web run e2e` | passed |

### Changes
- `app/sales/adapters/price_book.py`: `SqlPriceBook.for_variety` answers `None` for an archived variety. `record_sale` then raises `UnknownVariety`, so the answer is `sale.variety_not_found` and Nueva venta is untouched.
- Stock:
  - `ports.py`: `VarietyCatalog` gains `usable` and `archived_ids`.
  - `variety_catalog.py`: implements both.
  - `errors.py`: new code-less `VarietyArchived` signal.
  - `manage_entries.py`: `_check_variety` takes `keeps`. Recording requires `usable`. An edit that keeps its own variety needs only `exists`; an edit that changes variety needs `usable`.
  - `set_threshold.py`: requires `usable` for set and clear.
  - `read_stock.py`: takes `archived`, which picks the rows (`archived_ids` or `listed_ids`). The total still comes from the unfiltered load.
  - `router.py`: `GET /stock?archived=true`. The router re-raises the signal as varieties' `VarietyArchived` (`variety.archived`, 404) and registers that class in `as_http`.
- Catalog: `usable` on `CatalogVarieties`; code-less `CatalogVarietyArchived` signal; `set_variety_attributes` refuses an archived variety; the catalog router translates the signal to varieties' `VarietyArchived`. Missing still answers `catalog.variety_not_found`.
- Tests: `tests/fakes/archive.py` (`archive_by_hand`), then tests in each owner's test file. See Tests.

### Tests
All are HTTP tests over the real adapters and a fresh in-memory SQLite, at the declared seams:
- `tests/stock/test_stock_entries_api.py`: record on an archived variety refused; threshold set and clear refused; an edit keeps its own archived variety but cannot move onto another archived variety; an edit cannot move from a live variety onto an archived one; deleting an archived variety's entry works; an unknown variety still answers `variety.not_found`.
- `tests/stock/test_read_stock_api.py`: `?archived=true` rows; `total` and `entries` identical in both modes, and unchanged by archiving; `archived=false` gives the listed rows.
- `tests/catalog/test_catalog_api.py`: an archived variety is absent from the text and the list; `PUT` on it answers `variety.archived`; a missing id keeps `catalog.variety_not_found`.
- `tests/results/test_panel_router.py`: an archived variety leaves the panel stock rows while every other figure is identical.
- `tests/sales/test_sales_router.py`: `POST /sales` answers 404 `sale.variety_not_found` with 0 rows written; a sale of a variety archived afterwards can still be assigned a customer and deleted.
- `tests/test_business_isolation.py`: `/stock?archived=true` is in `LISTS`, plus a test that B reads none of A's archived rows.

### Decisions and assumptions
- I kept `CatalogVarieties.exists` and added `usable` beside it, because the router must tell missing from archived.
- Catalog and stock each have a code-less internal signal that their router re-raises as varieties' `VarietyArchived`, so `variety.archived` stays one class and `test_refusal_codes` is satisfied.
- `?archived=true` filters only the rows; `total` and `entries` stay whole.
- An edit "keeps" its variety when the posted `variety_id` equals the stored entry's.
- The isolation sweep gets both a `LISTS` entry and a dedicated test with an actually archived variety.

### For the Tech Lead
- Look first at `manage_entries._check_variety` (the `keeps` rule) and at the router's `_or_variety_not_found`.
- Not run: the full declared suite, `web-types`, `web-lint`, `web-tests`, `web-e2e`, and `ruff format --check`. A repo-wide `ruff format app tests` I ran by mistake rewrote many unrelated files; I reverted all of them and the reformatting of my own files (it had touched formatting in `tests/sales/test_sales_router.py` and `tests/test_business_isolation.py`), so the diff holds only intended changes.
- `ruff format --check` therefore still reports 2 files that would be reformatted (I did not identify which). Format is not among the declared checks, and I left them alone.
- The earlier findings on `a2-archive-restore` (migration test, `contract.json` duplication, RenameForm test) are not about this subtask and I did not touch them.

**What was checked against the diff `fb46393..d7a472e`**

- **Seam tests.** I ran `uv run --no-project pytest -n auto` on these files and every test passed, with no failures:
  - `tests/stock/test_stock_entries_api.py`
  - `tests/stock/test_read_stock_api.py`
  - `tests/catalog/test_catalog_api.py`
  - `tests/sales/test_sales_router.py`
  - `tests/results/test_panel_router.py`
  - `tests/test_business_isolation.py`
  - `tests/test_gated_routes.py`
- **Each brief item is in the code:**
  - `SqlPriceBook.for_variety` answers `None` for an archived variety.
  - `usable` exists on `VarietyCatalog` and on `CatalogVarieties`.
  - Recording an entry and setting or clearing a threshold require `usable`.
  - An entry edit uses `keeps=variety_id == existing.variety_id`, so it may stay on its own archived variety but needs `usable` when it changes variety.
  - Stock and catalog raise code-less internal signals, and their routers re-raise them as varieties' `VarietyArchived`.
  - A missing variety still answers `catalog.variety_not_found` in the catalog.
  - `GET /stock?archived=true` filters only the rows.
- **Each brief test exists and passes.** That includes the edit that keeps its archived variety, the edit refused when it moves onto another archived variety, deleting an entry and a sale, and assigning a customer.
- **Invariant 1.** No `all()` is used and no `include_archived` flag is added. `archived=true` reads only the archived varieties, which is the mode the plan defines.
- **Invariant 2.** `read_stock` still sums `total` over the whole `for_varieties(None)` load. A test asserts that `total` is the same before archiving, after archiving, and with `archived=true`. The panel test asserts that every figure except the stock rows is unchanged.
- **Invariant 6.** The diff does not touch `web/` or `contract.json`. `variety.archived` is already in the base at `web/src/api/contract.json:20`. No new refusal message interpolates a name or an amount.
- **Invariant 7.** No new route is added. The new query on `GET /stock` is in `LISTS`, and a dedicated test checks that business B reads none of A's archived rows. That test would fail if those rows leaked.
- **Invariant 8.** The diff contains no migration and no schema change, so the naming check in §11 has nothing to apply to.
- **Standards.** The sub-reviewer found no layering breach, no suppression and no personal data in error messages. The `tests/fakes/archive.py` helper is authorised by the brief.
- **Earlier findings.** The open rows belong to `a2-archive-restore`. This branch does not repeat them: it adds no contract entry and no migration.

### Changes
- Merge commit: A2 onto A1+C. A2's own `0012_variety_archived_at.py` and its migration test are removed, since A1's `0012_archived_variety.py` owns the column. A2's `NameHolder` in `ports.py` is removed and the domain's A1 enum is used.
- `app/varieties/adapters/sql_varieties.py`: `has_history`, `remove_price_entries` (Core delete), `remove_unused` (one `DELETE … AND NOT (history) RETURNING id`), and a private `_history_exists` of two `EXISTS` subqueries, each filtered on variety and business.
- `app/varieties/application/ports.py`, `manage_varieties.py`, `errors.py`: the new port methods, the `VarietyLeaving` protocol, `delete_variety` (get, archived gives `variety.archived`, history gives `variety.has_history` with nothing written, leaving, price entries, guarded delete, rollback and refusal if no row, one commit), `removal_offered`, and `VarietyHasHistory` with code `variety.has_history`.
- `app/varieties/adapters/router.py`, `schemas.py`: `DELETE /varieties/{id}` returning 204, and `GET /varieties/{id}` returning `VarietyDetailOut` with `removal`. The list stays without it.
- `app/stock/adapters/leaving.py`, `app/catalog/adapters/leaving.py`, `app/main.py`: each hexagon deletes its own row, composed as `app.state.variety_leaving`.
- `app/shared/reference.py`: the code-less `ReferenceRefused` signal. It is declared but unused.
- `web/src/api/contract.json`, `refusals.ts`: `variety.has_history` as a refusal, with fixed text.

### Tests
- `tests/varieties/test_delete_api.py`: HTTP seam. History from each of 8 sources alone (list line, gift, special price, four origins, both adjustment signs) refuses the delete with 409 and offers `archive`. A variety with no history offers `delete` and is deleted. Price, threshold and attributes alone leave `delete` offered and go with it, and the name is free again at once. Deleting the last entry or the last sale offers `delete` again. An archived variety offers `null` and its delete is 404 `variety.archived`. A restored variety whose history went away offers `delete`. A missing variety is 404 and the list carries no `removal`. Another business's archived variety is left alone.
- `tests/test_business_isolation.py`: DELETE added to the archived-variety parametrisation, plus a dedicated test for live and archived varieties. The route also joins the generic sweep and `tests/test_gated_routes.py` automatically, because both derive from OpenAPI.

### Decisions and assumptions
- Two classes may not share `variety.archived`: `tests/test_refusal_codes.py` fails on it. A2's application subclass is replaced by the single domain class, with an optional `variety_id`, re-exported from the application errors module.
- A2's archive round-trip test now expects `removal: None` for the archived page.
- The foreign-key-race brief items are not delivered, and so invariant 5's `IntegrityError`-to-gone translation is not written. The delivery ran out of turns before `ReferenceRefused` was wired into `sql_sales.py`, `sql_stock_entries.py` or the price-entry save. This is unwritten work, not an evidenced impossibility. The invariant 5 coverage line points only at the guarded-delete rollback that exists. The `variety.has_history` guard itself is race-safe through the single guarded statement.

### For the Tech Lead
- Look first at `SqlVarieties.remove_unused` and `delete_variety`.
- The remaining work for this subtask:
  - the `ReferenceRefused` wrapping in the three repositories, with the re-ask in `record_sale`, `record_entry`, `edit_entry` and `record_price`;
  - the two use-case tests for each re-ask branch;
  - the sqlite and libsql file-driver repository race tests.
- The previous presubmit failed one e2e spec (`analysis.spec.ts`, the "Variedad guardada" toast). It passes alone and my diff does not touch variety creation, so I take it as a flake under load.
- I did not run the full suite or the web checks. I ran ruff, mypy, and pytest on varieties, stock, catalog, sales and the refusal-codes test, all green.

+from sqlalchemy import ColumnElement, delete, exists, select
 from sqlalchemy.orm import Session, selectinload

 from app.shared.grams import Grams

-from app.varieties.application.ports import NameHolder

+from app.varieties.domain.variety import NameHolder, PriceEntry, Variety, VarietyName
 
 
 class SqlVarieties:
@@ -72,6 +73,43 @@ class SqlVarieties:
             return None
         return NameHolder.LIVE if rows[0].archived_at is None else NameHolder.ARCHIVED
 
+    def has_history(self, variety_id: int) -> bool:
+        return bool(self._session.scalar(select(self._history_exists(variety_id))))
+
+    def remove_price_entries(self, variety_id: int) -> None:
+        self._session.execute(
+            delete(PriceEntryRow).where(
+                PriceEntryRow.variety_id == variety_id,
+                PriceEntryRow.business_id == self._business_id,
+            )
+        )
+        self._session.expire_all()
+
+    def remove_unused(self, variety_id: int) -> bool:
+        """One statement: the history check and the delete cannot be pulled apart by a
+        sale or an entry recorded in between (the foreign keys close the rest)."""
+        statement = (
+            delete(VarietyRow)
+            .where(
+                VarietyRow.id == variety_id,
+                VarietyRow.business_id == self._business_id,
+                ~self._history_exists(variety_id),
+            )
+            .returning(VarietyRow.id)
+        )
+        removed = self._session.execute(statement).first()
+        self._session.expire_all()
+        return removed is not None
+
+    def _history_exists(self, variety_id: int) -> ColumnElement[bool]:
+        lines = exists().where(
+            SaleLineRow.variety_id == variety_id, SaleLineRow.business_id == self._business_id
+        )
+        entries = exists().where(
+            StockEntryRow.variety_id == variety_id, StockEntryRow.business_id == self._business_id
+        )
+        return lines | entries
+
     def _row(self, variety_id: int) -> VarietyRow | None:
         statement = select(VarietyRow).where(
             VarietyRow.id == variety_id, VarietyRow.business_id == self._business_id

+from app.varieties.application.errors import (
+    NameHeldByArchived,
+    NameTaken,
+    VarietyArchived,

+)

 def register_variety(varieties: Varieties, transaction: Transaction, raw_name: str) -> Variety:
@@ -62,3 +69,34 @@ def get_variety(varieties: Varieties, variety_id: int) -> Variety:
 
 def list_varieties(varieties: Varieties, *, archived: bool = False) -> list[Variety]:
     return varieties.archived() if archived else varieties.listed()
+
+
+def delete_variety(
+    varieties: Varieties,
+    leaving: VarietyLeaving,
+    transaction: Transaction,
+    variety_id: int,
+) -> None:
+    """Only a variety with no history is deleted: the common refusal writes nothing, and
+    the last word is one guarded delete after the dependents, in one transaction."""
+    variety = get_variety(varieties, variety_id)
+    if variety.is_archived:
+        raise VarietyArchived(variety_id)
+    if varieties.has_history(variety_id):
+        raise VarietyHasHistory
+    leaving.leaving(variety_id)
+    varieties.remove_price_entries(variety_id)
+    if not varieties.remove_unused(variety_id):
+        transaction.rollback()
+        raise VarietyHasHistory
+    transaction.commit()
+
+
+def removal_offered(
+    varieties: Varieties, variety: Variety
+) -> Literal["delete", "archive"] | None:
+    """`None` for an archived variety; otherwise the one removal its page offers."""
+    if variety.is_archived:
+        return None
+    assert variety.id is not None
+    return "archive" if varieties.has_history(variety.id) else "delete"

+def _require_name_free(varieties: Varieties, name: VarietyName, other_than: int | None) -> None:
+    holder = varieties.name_holder(name, other_than=other_than)
+    if holder is NameHolder.ARCHIVED:
+        raise NameHeldByArchived
+    if holder is NameHolder.LIVE:
+        raise NameTaken(name.value)

+def archive_variety(
+    varieties: Varieties, transaction: Transaction, variety_id: int, at: datetime
+) -> Variety:
+    """Never refused because the variety has or lacks history: only deleting is guarded."""
+    variety = get_variety(varieties, variety_id)
+    variety.archive(at)
+    stored = varieties.save(variety)
+    transaction.commit()
+    return stored
+
+

-def list_varieties(varieties: Varieties) -> list[Variety]:
-    return varieties.listed()
+def list_varieties(varieties: Varieties, *, archived: bool = False) -> list[Variety]:
+    return varieties.archived() if archived else varieties.listed()
+
+
+def delete_variety(
+    varieties: Varieties,
+    leaving: VarietyLeaving,
+    transaction: Transaction,
+    variety_id: int,
+) -> None:
+    """Only a variety with no history is deleted: the common refusal writes nothing, and
+    the last word is one guarded delete after the dependents, in one transaction."""
+    variety = get_variety(varieties, variety_id)
+    if variety.is_archived:
+        raise VarietyArchived(variety_id)
+    if varieties.has_history(variety_id):
+        raise VarietyHasHistory
+    leaving.leaving(variety_id)
+    varieties.remove_price_entries(variety_id)
+    if not varieties.remove_unused(variety_id):
+        transaction.rollback()
+        raise VarietyHasHistory
+    transaction.commit()
+
+
+def removal_offered(
+    varieties: Varieties, variety: Variety
+) -> Literal["delete", "archive"] | None:
+    """`None` for an archived variety; otherwise the one removal its page offers."""
+    if variety.is_archived:
+        return None
+    assert variety.id is not None
+    return "archive" if varieties.has_history(variety.id) else "delete"

+
+
+def _bare_gold() -> Variety:
+    return Variety(VarietyName.of("24K Gold"), id=1)
+
+
+def test_archiving_sets_the_instant_once_and_a_second_archive_keeps_it() -> None:
+    variety = _bare_gold()
+
+    variety.archive(datetime(2026, 10, 6, 12, 0, tzinfo=UTC))
+    variety.archive(datetime(2026, 10, 7, 12, 0, tzinfo=UTC))
+
+    assert variety.is_archived
+    assert variety.archived_at == datetime(2026, 10, 6, 12, 0, tzinfo=UTC)
+
+
+def test_restoring_clears_the_instant_and_restoring_a_live_variety_changes_nothing() -> None:
+    variety = _bare_gold()
+    variety.restore()
+    assert not variety.is_archived
+
+    variety.archive(datetime(2026, 10, 6, 12, 0, tzinfo=UTC))
+    variety.restore()
+
+    assert not variety.is_archived
+    assert variety.archived_at is None
+
+
+def test_an_archived_variety_refuses_to_be_renamed_priced_or_unpriced() -> None:
+    variety = _bare_gold()
+    variety.record_price(PriceEntry.of(date(2026, 1, 1), 11_000, id=5))
+    variety.archive(datetime(2026, 10, 6, 12, 0, tzinfo=UTC))
+
+    with pytest.raises(VarietyArchived):
+        variety.rename(VarietyName.of("Silver"))
+    with pytest.raises(VarietyArchived):
+        variety.record_price(PriceEntry.of(date(2026, 2, 1), 12_000))
+    with pytest.raises(VarietyArchived):
+        variety.delete_price(5)
+
+    assert variety.name.value == "24K Gold"
+    assert len(variety.price_entries) == 1
+
+
+def test_a_restored_variety_can_be_renamed_again() -> None:
+    variety = _bare_gold()
+    variety.archive(datetime(2026, 10, 6, 12, 0, tzinfo=UTC))
+    variety.restore()
+
+    variety.rename(VarietyName.of("Silver"))
+
+    assert variety.name.value == "Silver"

## Developer: b-delete (v2)

### Checks

Run by the system on `fdd6d24` when the Developer delivered.

| Check | Command | Result |
|---|---|---|
| `tests` | `uv run --no-project pytest -n auto` | passed |
| `lint` | `uv run --no-project ruff check .` | passed |
| `types` | `uv run --no-project mypy app tests` | passed |
| `web-types` | `npm --prefix web run typecheck` | passed |
| `web-lint` | `npm --prefix web run lint` | passed |
| `web-tests` | `npm --prefix web test` | passed |
| `web-e2e` | `npm --prefix web run e2e` | passed |

### Changes
- `app/shared/reference.py`: the code-less `ReferenceRefused` signal.
- `app/db.py`: `flush_or_refuse(session)` flushes and raises `ReferenceRefused` on `IntegrityError`, and on libsql's bare `ValueError("FOREIGN KEY constraint failed")`. Any other `ValueError` propagates. The signal is raised outside the `except`.
- `app/sales/adapters/sql_sales.py`, `app/stock/adapters/sql_stock_entries.py` (`add` and `save`) and `app/varieties/adapters/sql_varieties.py` (`save`) flush through it. `SqlVarieties.save` also raises the signal when its row is gone.
- `app/sales/application/record_sale.py`, `app/stock/application/manage_entries.py` (`record_entry`, `edit_entry`) and `app/varieties/application/manage_prices.py` (`record_price_entry`) catch the signal and roll back. They then re-ask their own lookup and raise the gone code (`sale.variety_not_found`, `variety.not_found` or `variety.archived`), or re-raise when the variety is still usable.
- `app/varieties/adapters/sql_varieties.py` holds the guarded delete. `remove_unused` is one `DELETE … AND NOT (history) RETURNING id` filtered on business, and `remove_price_entries` and `has_history` sit beside it.
- `app/varieties/application/manage_varieties.py`: `delete_variety` checks existence, archived and history, then runs the leaving parts, `remove_price_entries` and `remove_unused`, with one commit. If the guarded delete removes nothing it rolls back and answers `VarietyHasHistory`. `removal_offered` serves the page.
- `app/varieties/application/errors.py`, `ports.py`, `adapters/router.py`, `adapters/schemas.py`: `VarietyHasHistory` (409), the `VarietyLeaving` port, `DELETE /varieties/{id}` (204), and `GET /varieties/{id}` returning `removal`.
- `app/stock/adapters/leaving.py` and `app/catalog/adapters/leaving.py` remove the threshold and the catalog attributes, and `app/main.py` composes them.
- `web/src/api/contract.json` and `web/src/api/refusals.ts` carry `variety.has_history` and its fixed message.

### Tests
- `tests/varieties/test_delete_api.py`: the HTTP matrix covering each source of history alone, no history, prices, threshold and attributes going with the delete, an archived variety, a restored variety, a missing variety, and another business's data.
- `tests/varieties/test_reference_refused.py`: use-case tests with fakes for `record_sale`, `record_entry`, `edit_entry` and `record_price_entry`. Each checks the gone branch, the archived branch where it applies, and the re-raise branch, plus one rollback and no commit. Unit tests for `flush_or_refuse` cover the libsql `ValueError` and a non-FK `ValueError` that must propagate.
- `tests/varieties/test_sql_varieties_delete.py`: real `sqlite:///` and `sqlite+libsql:///` files with two connections.
  - `remove_unused` with and without history, and for another business.
  - Dependents survive a rollback.
  - `delete_variety` rolls back when the guarded delete removes nothing.
  - Insert first: a partner's entry lands after the history check, and the delete answers `has_history`.
  - Delete first: the entry and the price answer gone, and no orphans are left in the five tables that reference `variety`.
- `tests/test_business_isolation.py`: DELETE joins the archived parametrization, plus another business cannot delete a variety with no history.
- `tests/varieties/test_archive_api.py`: the archive round-trip now expects `removal: None`.

### Decisions and assumptions
- `VarietyArchived` is one class for `variety.archived` (the domain class, re-exported), so `tests/test_refusal_codes.py` keeps one class per code.
- libsql does not raise `IntegrityError` for a foreign-key failure: it raises a bare `ValueError`. `flush_or_refuse` matches on the message and re-raises anything else, so a different `ValueError` is not hidden as a refusal.
- No `importorskip` on libsql: a missing driver fails the tests.
- The earlier e2e failure (`analysis.spec.ts`) was a flake and passed alone.

### For the Tech Lead
- Look first at `app/db.py:101` and the three catch sites. Each rolls back before the re-ask lookup, and the `IntegrityError` is never converted to a gone code without that lookup confirming it.
- The race tests inject the partner's write through subclasses and stale-read wrappers, so both orders are deterministic on a real file with two connections.
- I did not run the web checks or e2e myself, only ruff, mypy and the full pytest. The e2e run belongs to the delivery gate.
- The review's finding about the seed in `tests/varieties/test_archive_moves_no_figure.py` is outside this subtask.

### Verdict

**returned** (`plan-invariant`): Invariant 5 and the foreign-key race part of the brief are missing: ReferenceRefused is never raised or caught, and the repository race tests and use-case re-ask tests don't exist. The Developer's own record says so.

### Findings

| severity | category | location | claim | evidence |

| blocking | plan-invariant | `app/shared/reference.py:9` | Invariant 5 is not met. No repository turns an `IntegrityError` into `ReferenceRefused`, and `record_sale`, `record_entry`, `edit_entry` and `record_price` never roll back and re-ask their lookup. A sale, entry or price that races a delete still comes back as a raw 500. | `grep -rn "ReferenceRefused\|IntegrityError" app` finds only the class definition, plus the unrelated expenses and customers adapters. The Developer's Decisions say: "unwritten work, not an evidenced impossibility". |
| blocking | spec-missing-test | `tests/varieties` | Two kinds of tests at declared seams are missing: use-case tests where a fake raises `ReferenceRefused` (gone and present branches), and `SqlVarieties` tests over sqlite and libsql files (`remove_unused` and the two-connection race in both orders). | `pytest -n auto tests -k "remove_unused or race or reference_refused or ReferenceRefused or libsql_delete" -rA` gives `2 passed`, both unrelated `*no_traceback` tests. `grep -rln "ReferenceRefused\|remove_unused" tests` finds nothing. Brief, `Seams:` line. |
| blocking | spec-missing-test | `app/varieties/application/manage_varieties.py:88` | Nothing tests the rollback branch of `delete_variety`, where `remove_unused` returns `False` after the dependent deletes. The brief asks for a test that the dependents survive it. | Brief, Tests: "after a forced rollback following the dependent deletes, thresholds, attributes and price entries are all still there". |
| observation | process-suite-red | `web/e2e/analysis.spec.ts` | The Developer reports an earlier e2e failure and calls it a flake. The system's web-e2e run on `fd70aeb` passed. | none |

What I verified as correct:
- **Guarded delete.** `remove_unused` is one `DELETE … AND NOT (EXISTS sale_line OR EXISTS stock_entry) RETURNING id`, filtered on the business. It runs after `leaving` and `remove_price_entries`, in one transaction with one commit (invariant 4). `PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON` is set in `app/db.py:89`.
- **Archive.** Archive is never refused because of history (invariant 3).
- **Readers.** Readers go through `listed()`, `archived()` and `get()` (invariant 1).
- **Refusal codes.** `variety.has_history` and `variety.name_archived` are each added once, with fixed text in `refusals.ts` (invariant 6).
- **Isolation.** The new DELETE route takes `CurrentBusinessId` and joins the isolation and gated-route tests (invariant 7). Those suites and `tests/varieties/test_delete_api.py` are green here.
- **Schema.** This diff makes no schema change, so the §11 naming check does not apply.
- **Open rows on `contract.json:20`.** These are settled: `variety.archived` appears once and only `0012_archived_variety.py` exists.
- **Open row on the A2 migration test.** That row is settled: the file was removed in the merge.

### For the Developer

1. Wrap the flush in `sql_sales.py` (the record path), `sql_stock_entries.py` (entry add and save) and the varieties price-entry save.
   - Catch `IntegrityError` only to set a flag, then raise `ReferenceRefused` outside the `except`.
   - In `record_sale`, `record_entry`, `edit_entry` and `record_price`, catch it, call `transaction.rollback()` and re-ask your own lookup.
   - If the variety is gone or archived, raise its gone code (`sale.variety_not_found`, `variety.not_found` or `variety.archived`). Otherwise re-raise the original error.
   - To verify: `grep -rn ReferenceRefused app` shows a raise in the three repositories and a catch in the four use cases.
2. Add use-case tests for all four use cases, each with a fake whose flush raises `ReferenceRefused`: one where the lookup says gone (gone code expected) and one where it says present (the error is re-raised).
3. Add `SqlVarieties` tests over real `sqlite:///` and `sqlite+libsql:///` files:
   - `remove_unused` returns the id with no history and nothing with history;
   - after a forced rollback following the dependent deletes, the threshold, attributes and price entries are all still there;
   - a two-connection race in both orders: delete first means the insert is refused as gone; insert first means the delete answers `variety.has_history`. 0 rows reference a missing variety.

**returned** (`spec-missing-test`): The sale-line race has no test over real SQLite files, though the PRD metric names it. sql_sales.py's new flush_or_refuse is only tested with a fake that raises the signal, never on a real foreign-key failure, on either driver or in either order.

| severity | category | location | claim | evidence |

So `sql_sales.py`'s new `flush_or_refuse` never meets a real foreign-key failure. | `pytest -n auto tests/varieties/test_sql_varieties_delete.py -k sale -rA` selects no test. PRD Metrics: "a repository test over real SQLite races a delete against a sale line and against a stock entry". Brief: "a two-connection race in both orders". |
| observation | spec-behaviour | `app/varieties/adapters/sql_varieties.py:55` | `save` now raises `ReferenceRefused` for a row that is gone, but only `record_price_entry` catches it. Rename, archive and restore still answer 500 if they race a delete, as the old assert did, so nothing regressed. | `grep -rn "except ReferenceRefused" app` shows 4 catch sites. |
| observation | standard-other | `tests/varieties/test_archive_api.py:86` | Reformatting churn in two test files, with no change in behaviour. | none |

What I verified as correct:
- **Earlier item 1 (invariant 5).** `flush_or_refuse` (`app/db.py:101`) catches `IntegrityError`, and libsql's bare foreign-key `ValueError`, only to set a flag. It raises `ReferenceRefused` outside the `except`, and any other `ValueError` propagates.
  - The sales record path, the stock entry `add` and `save`, and the varieties `save` all flush through it.
  - `record_sale`, `record_entry`, `edit_entry` and `record_price_entry` catch the signal and roll back.
  - Each then re-asks its own lookup and raises `sale.variety_not_found`, `variety.not_found` or `variety.archived`, or re-raises when the variety is still usable. `edit_entry` keeps its own archived variety (`keeps=`).
- **Earlier item 2.** `tests/varieties/test_reference_refused.py` covers all four use cases in the gone branch, the archived branch where it applies, and the re-raise branch. Each checks one rollback and no commit. Both `flush_or_refuse` `ValueError` cases are covered too.
- **Earlier item 3, entries and prices.** These parts are done:
  - `remove_unused` is checked with and without history and for another business.
  - The dependents survive a rollback.
  - `delete_variety` rolls back when the guarded delete removes nothing.
  - Entry-first answers `has_history`.
  - Delete-first makes the entry and the price answer gone, and leaves 0 orphans.
  
  All of this runs on both `sqlite` and `sqlite+libsql` files: `pytest -n auto tests/varieties/test_sql_varieties_delete.py tests/varieties/test_reference_refused.py` gives 29 passed.
- **Other invariants.** Invariants 1, 3, 4, 6 and 7 are untouched by this delta and stay as approved. The diff changes no schema, so the §11 naming check does not apply.

1. In `tests/varieties/test_sql_varieties_delete.py`, add the sale-line race over both file drivers, in both orders:
   - **Delete first:** a `record_sale` whose price book read the variety before the partner's delete answers `UnknownVariety` (`sale.variety_not_found`). Afterwards `sale` and `sale_line` hold no new row and `_orphans` is 0.
   - **Sale first:** a sale line committed by a partner connection after `has_history` makes `delete_variety` raise `VarietyHasHistory`. The variety and its dependents are still there.

## Tech Lead: review b-delete

### Verdict

**approved**: I checked the one fix the last review asked for. The sale-line race now runs over real sqlite and libsql files in both orders, and all 4 tests pass. The delete-first test reaches the gone code only through a real foreign-key failure at sql_sales.py's flush, and both orders check that 0 rows are orphaned.

### Findings

| severity | category | location | claim | evidence |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| — | — | — | No new findings. | — |

The one item from the previous pass is fixed:

- **Item 1, the sale-line race on real files.** `tests/varieties/test_sql_varieties_delete.py:323-400` adds the race in both orders. Each order runs over both the `sqlite` and the `sqlite+libsql` file drivers, through the `engine` fixture's two params.
  - `uv run --no-project pytest -n auto tests/varieties/test_sql_varieties_delete.py -k sale -rA` gives 4 passed, one for each order on each driver.
  - **Delete first.** The request prices the variety, then a partner commits the delete. `_PricesStaleOnce` returns the stale pricing only once, so `_resolve_line` does not raise. The only way the test can see `UnknownVariety` is through `SqlSales.add`'s `flush_or_refuse` failing on a real foreign key. `record_sale` then rolls back, asks the price book again, and raises the gone code (`record_sale.py:66-73`). The test also checks that `sale` and `sale_line` are empty and that `_orphans` is 0; `DEPENDENTS` includes `sale_line`.
  - **Sale first.** A partner commits a gift sale after `has_history`. `delete_variety` then raises `VarietyHasHistory`, and the variety, the sale line, the price entry, the threshold and the catalog attributes all survive, with 0 orphans.
- **Rest of the diff.** It touches only the test file, so invariants 1–8 and the production code stay as approved. There is no schema change, so the §11 naming check does not apply. `### Checks` on `18a11aa` is all green.

Open rows:
- I left open the `e-books-unchanged` row about the seed in `tests/varieties/test_archive_moves_no_figure.py`. It belongs to another subtask, and this branch neither touches that file nor repeats the problem.
- The observation on `sql_varieties.py:55` is unchanged by this diff and stays open.

### For the Developer

## Developer: e-books-unchanged

### Checks

Run by the system on `82d24f6` when the Developer delivered.

| Check | Command | Result |
|---|---|---|
| `tests` | `uv run --no-project pytest -n auto` | passed |
| `lint` | `uv run --no-project ruff check .` | passed |
| `types` | `uv run --no-project mypy app tests` | passed |
| `web-types` | `npm --prefix web run typecheck` | passed |
| `web-lint` | `npm --prefix web run lint` | passed |
| `web-tests` | `npm --prefix web test` | passed |
| `web-e2e` | `npm --prefix web run e2e` | passed |

### Changes
- Finished the merge of `a2-archive-restore` into this c-based branch. a2 carried its own copy of A1's work, so I kept A1's side where they overlapped.
- Removed `app/migrations/versions/0012_variety_archived_at.py` and `tests/migrations/test_variety_archived_at.py`, a2's duplicates of A1's `0012_archived_variety.py` and `test_0012_archived_variety.py`. The migration and its test now exist once. This also settles the `importorskip` observation, because the libsql case lives in A1's test file.
- `app/varieties/domain/variety.py`: `VarietyArchived` takes an optional `variety_id`.
- `app/varieties/application/errors.py`: removed the second `VarietyArchived` class, so `variety.archived` has one class and `tests/test_refusal_codes.py` passes again. It re-exports the domain class for the stock and catalog routers, and the doubled `__init__` on `NameHeldByArchived` is gone.
- `app/varieties/adapters/router.py`: dropped the unused `ArchivedForNewRecord` alias.
- `tests/varieties/test_variety.py`: fixed a missing blank line left by the merge.
- `tests/varieties/test_archive_moves_no_figure.py` is new. It is test code only.

### Tests
All at the declared HTTP seams, with the `client` fixture and the movable clock.
- `test_no_figure_of_the_books_moves_when_a_variety_is_archived_and_restored`:
  - Seeded through the API: purchases, a sale left owing with a later payment, a cash sale, a gift, a special price, a costed negative adjustment, an overdrawing negative adjustment, a sale line with pending grams, and an expense.
  - Captured before archiving, while archived and after restoring: `/analysis` for 2026-09 and 2026-10, `/panel` periods, `/sales` and every `/sales/{id}`, `/customers`, the customer account, and `/stock` total and entries. All three captures are equal.
  - The archived variety's stock row is the same before and after, and the same when read from `GET /stock?archived=true` while archived.
- `test_a_restored_variety_comes_back_whole`, parametrised hidden and shown:
  - Prices, entries, stock row, threshold and catalog attributes are equal before archiving and after restoring.
  - The variety is out of `/varieties`, `/stock`, `/catalog` and the panel's stock rows while archived, and back in them after.
- `test_archive_and_restore_edit_the_pin_exactly_when_the_text_changes`: one edit on archive and one on restore, using the existing fake Telegram.
- `test_archiving_a_hidden_or_unpriced_variety_sends_nothing`: the input that must not trigger an edit.
- `test_the_triggering_request_answers_normally_when_telegram_fails`: archive still answers 200 when the fake fails.

### Decisions and assumptions
- Kept A1's migration and test over a2's copies, so the migration is registered once.
- Unified the two same-code `VarietyArchived` classes into the domain one, because the refusal-code test requires one class per code.
- No production figure moved, so there was no fix to land with the capture test.

### For the Tech Lead
- Check the merge commit `c3972c0` first: it resolves the findings about the duplicate migration and the duplicate `variety.archived` entry. The conflicted files had no markers left when I opened them, but they were still unresolved and several were inconsistent. I resolved them file by file.
- The capture list in `_books` is the proof of criterion 2, as the plan's risk note says.
- `ruff format --check` is not a declared check and reports pre-existing diffs across the repo; I did not touch them.

+def _require_name_free(varieties: Varieties, name: VarietyName, other_than: int | None) -> None:
+    holder = varieties.name_holder(name, other_than=other_than)
+    if holder is NameHolder.ARCHIVED:

+    if holder is NameHolder.LIVE:

+def archive_variety(
+    varieties: Varieties, transaction: Transaction, variety_id: int, at: datetime
+) -> Variety:
+    """Never refused because the variety has or lacks history: only deleting is guarded."""
+    variety = get_variety(varieties, variety_id)
+    variety.archive(at)
+    stored = varieties.save(variety)
+    transaction.commit()
+    return stored
+
+
+def restore_variety(varieties: Varieties, transaction: Transaction, variety_id: int) -> Variety:
+    variety = get_variety(varieties, variety_id)
+    variety.restore()

@@ -126,10 +136,25 @@ class Variety:

 """The rules of a variety and its price history, with no infrastructure."""
 
-from datetime import date
+from datetime import UTC, date, datetime
 
 import pytest

@@ -141,3 +142,55 @@ def test_a_variety_is_live_until_it_carries_an_archived_instant() -> None:
     assert live.archived_at is None
     assert not live.is_archived
     assert archived.is_archived
+
+
+def _bare_gold() -> Variety:
+    return Variety(VarietyName.of("24K Gold"), id=1)
+
+
+def test_archiving_sets_the_instant_once_and_a_second_archive_keeps_it() -> None:
+    variety = _bare_gold()
+
+    variety.archive(datetime(2026, 10, 6, 12, 0, tzinfo=UTC))
+    variety.archive(datetime(2026, 10, 7, 12, 0, tzinfo=UTC))
+
+    assert variety.is_archived
+    assert variety.archived_at == datetime(2026, 10, 6, 12, 0, tzinfo=UTC)
+
+
+def test_restoring_clears_the_instant_and_restoring_a_live_variety_changes_nothing() -> None:
+    variety = _bare_gold()
+    variety.restore()
+    assert not variety.is_archived
+
+    variety.archive(datetime(2026, 10, 6, 12, 0, tzinfo=UTC))
+    variety.restore()
+
+    assert not variety.is_archived
+    assert variety.archived_at is None
+
+
+def test_an_archived_variety_refuses_to_be_renamed_priced_or_unpriced() -> None:
+    variety = _bare_gold()
+    variety.record_price(PriceEntry.of(date(2026, 1, 1), 11_000, id=5))
+    variety.archive(datetime(2026, 10, 6, 12, 0, tzinfo=UTC))
+
+    with pytest.raises(VarietyArchived):
+        variety.rename(VarietyName.of("Silver"))
+    with pytest.raises(VarietyArchived):
+        variety.record_price(PriceEntry.of(date(2026, 2, 1), 12_000))
+    with pytest.raises(VarietyArchived):
+        variety.delete_price(5)
+
+    assert variety.name.value == "24K Gold"
+    assert len(variety.price_entries) == 1
+
+
+def test_a_restored_variety_can_be_renamed_again() -> None:
+    variety = _bare_gold()
+    variety.archive(datetime(2026, 10, 6, 12, 0, tzinfo=UTC))
+    variety.restore()
+
+    variety.rename(VarietyName.of("Silver"))
+
+    assert variety.name.value == "Silver"

## Tech Lead: review e-books-unchanged

### Verdict

**approved**: Verified that the capture, comes-back-whole and upkeep tests pass and cover the brief, that `variety.archived` and migration 0012 are each registered once after the merge, and that the new routes are in the gated-route test and the isolation sweep.

### Findings

| severity | category | location | claim | evidence |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| important | spec-missing-test | `tests/varieties/test_archive_moves_no_figure.py:61` | The panel's `today` and `week` periods hold no data in any capture: the clock is set to 10-15 and the last sale is on 10-03. A filter on archived varieties in those two periods would go unseen; only `month` is exercised. | Clock at :61 is 2026-10-15; sales at :71/:77/:81/:100 run from 09-12 to 10-03; :155 asserts only `month`. The test file passes. |

Notes from verification:
- **Tests run green.** `uv run --no-project pytest -n auto` passed over `tests/varieties/test_archive_moves_no_figure.py`, `tests/varieties/test_archive_api.py`, `tests/test_gated_routes.py`, `tests/test_business_isolation.py`, `tests/test_refusal_codes.py` and `tests/migrations/test_0012_archived_variety.py`.
- **Invariant 6.** `web/src/api/contract.json` now lists `variety.archived` once (line 20) and `variety.name_archived` once (line 22), with its `refusals.ts` entry. `errors.py` re-exports the single domain `VarietyArchived`, which interpolates nothing. Migration 0012 exists once; a2's duplicate file is gone.
- **Invariant 7.** The archive and restore routes are covered:
  - `test_gated_routes.py` reads every route from OpenAPI.
  - `test_business_isolation.py:910-933` sweeps both routes from another business and gets 404 `variety.not_found`.
- **Invariants 1–5 and 8.** This diff adds no reader, no delete, no use case and no migration, so it has nothing to break there. No schema changed, so §11 naming does not apply.
- **The brief, item by item:**
  - The capture covers analysis for both months of the history, panel periods, every sale, the sales list, customers, the account, stock total and entries, and the archived stock row through `?archived=true`.
  - Comes back whole runs hidden and shown. `/catalog` lists every unarchived variety with its `shown` flag and attributes (`read_catalog.py:_qualify`), so the comparison is not vacuous.
  - Upkeep edits exactly when the text changes. A hidden or unpriced variety sends nothing. A failing Telegram still answers 200.
- **Open row not settled.** The `importorskip` row (a2, `tests/migrations/test_variety_archived_at.py:42`) stays open. That file was removed, but the same `pytest.importorskip("sqlalchemy_libsql")` is still in A1's `tests/migrations/test_0012_archived_variety.py:168`, which this diff does not touch.
- **The `code-review` skill could not be loaded:** it is not on this session's allowlist. I ran both axes directly in this checkout.

### For the Developer

## Developer: d1-removal-action (v1)

### Checks

Run by the system on `8cfcd34` when the Developer delivered.

| Check | Command | Result |
|---|---|---|
| `tests` | `uv run --no-project pytest -n auto` | passed |
| `lint` | `uv run --no-project ruff check .` | passed |
| `types` | `uv run --no-project mypy app tests` | passed |
| `web-types` | `npm --prefix web run typecheck` | passed |
| `web-lint` | `npm --prefix web run lint` | passed |
| `web-tests` | `npm --prefix web test` | passed |
| `web-e2e` | `npm --prefix web run e2e` | passed |

### Changes

- **Merge (e9eac4a):** resolved b-delete's conflicts file by file. `errors.py`, `ports.py`, `manage_varieties.py` and `router.py` take B's additions on top of A2's. `sql_varieties.py` keeps A2's `name_holder` and `_by_name` plus B's `has_history`, `remove_price_entries`, `remove_unused` and `_history_exists`. `ventasPhone.spec.ts` takes our side. e-books-unchanged was already in HEAD.
- `web/src/api/varieties.ts` (new): `VarietyDetail` with `archived` and `removal`, and `archiveVariety`, `restoreVariety`, `deleteVariety`.
- `web/src/screens/varieties/VarietyRemoval.tsx` (new):
  - the one removal action, from `removal`;
  - the destructive delete dialog and the archive dialog with the grams wording;
  - toasts and error handling;
  - computer reselection.
- `VarietyPage.tsx` renders it at the foot. `Variedades.tsx` gains `archived` on `Variety`. `AvisoExito.tsx` gains the two toasts. `app.css` gains `.variedad-retiro`.
- `EntradaStock.tsx`: when editing an entry whose variety is not in the listed set, it asks `GET /varieties?archived=true` and adds only that variety to the picker. `keys.ts` gains `archivedVarieties`.

### Tests

- `VarietyRemoval.test.tsx`, at the VarietyPage seam:
  - the action for each `removal` value (including `null`);
  - both confirmations, the grams wording, zero stock and cancel sending nothing;
  - delete and archive toasts and the return to Variedades;
  - the stale-delete refresh with no toast;
  - the gone notice for `variety.not_found` and `variety.archived`;
  - the offline case;
  - computer reselection: next, previous, the only one left;
  - phone returns to the list;
  - no name or amount in the toast, console or storage.
- `EntradaStock.test.tsx`: editing an archived variety's entry shows it chosen and offers no other archived one; a new entry never asks for the archived list.
- Python varieties and isolation tests (`pytest -n auto tests/varieties tests/test_business_isolation.py`) pass after the merge.

### Decisions and assumptions

See `assumptions`. The main ones: no name in dialogs, the wording for the delete dialog and negative stock, the cache edit and `replace` navigation, and computer reselection from the cached list.

### For the Tech Lead

- Start with `VarietyRemoval.tsx` (`leave()` and `onConfirm()`).
- The design files have no removal action, so the button is a soft full-width button at the foot and its look is unreviewed. The comparison ran with no notes.
- `web-e2e` and the full Python suite were not run in this turn. I ran `pytest -n auto tests/varieties tests/test_business_isolation.py`, ruff and mypy once after the merge; web typecheck, lint and vitest were green.
- The Python tree has `ruff format --check` differences in 70 files; none comes from this subtask and format is not a declared check.

+    def has_history(self, variety_id: int) -> bool:

+    def remove_price_entries(self, variety_id: int) -> None:

+            delete(PriceEntryRow).where(
+                PriceEntryRow.variety_id == variety_id,
+                PriceEntryRow.business_id == self._business_id,
+            )
+        )

+
+    def remove_unused(self, variety_id: int) -> bool:
+        """One statement: the history check and the delete cannot be pulled apart by a
+        sale or an entry recorded in between (the foreign keys close the rest)."""
+        statement = (
+            delete(VarietyRow)
+            .where(
+                VarietyRow.id == variety_id,
+                VarietyRow.business_id == self._business_id,
+                ~self._history_exists(variety_id),
+            )
+            .returning(VarietyRow.id)
+        )
+        removed = self._session.execute(statement).first()
+        self._session.expire_all()
+        return removed is not None
+

+        lines = exists().where(
+            SaleLineRow.variety_id == variety_id, SaleLineRow.business_id == self._business_id
+        )
+        entries = exists().where(
+            StockEntryRow.variety_id == variety_id, StockEntryRow.business_id == self._business_id
+        )
+        return lines | entries
+

         statement = select(VarietyRow).where(
             VarietyRow.id == variety_id, VarietyRow.business_id == self._business_id

 def list_varieties(varieties: Varieties, *, archived: bool = False) -> list[Variety]:

+
+
+def delete_variety(
+    varieties: Varieties,
+    leaving: VarietyLeaving,
+    transaction: Transaction,
+    variety_id: int,
+) -> None:
+    """Only a variety with no history is deleted: the common refusal writes nothing, and
+    the last word is one guarded delete after the dependents, in one transaction."""
+    variety = get_variety(varieties, variety_id)
+    if variety.is_archived:
+        raise VarietyArchived(variety_id)
+    if varieties.has_history(variety_id):
+        raise VarietyHasHistory
+    leaving.leaving(variety_id)
+    varieties.remove_price_entries(variety_id)
+    if not varieties.remove_unused(variety_id):
+        transaction.rollback()
+        raise VarietyHasHistory
+    transaction.commit()
+
+
+def removal_offered(
+    varieties: Varieties, variety: Variety
+) -> Literal["delete", "archive"] | None:
+    """`None` for an archived variety; otherwise the one removal its page offers."""
+    if variety.is_archived:
+        return None
+    assert variety.id is not None
+    return "archive" if varieties.has_history(variety.id) else "delete"

The app start (`scripts/design/sample-app.sh`) printed nothing and this seat could not hit a server, so I walked the built front in `web/src/` screen by screen against the validated flow. HEAD is `59ad57b`, *"Record that d2-archivadas is blocked: pantallas/ has no Archivadas card or archived page"*.

**Job 1: delete a variety that never moved**
- *Open the variety*: unchanged Variedades → VarietyPage.
- *"Eliminar variedad"*: rendered by `VarietyRemoval` at the end of the page, only when `removal === 'delete'`.
- *Destructive confirm*: "¿Eliminás esta variedad?" / "No tiene ventas ni entradas. Se borra para siempre, con sus precios…" / "No, dejala" · "Sí, eliminala".
- *Back*: the toast "Variedad eliminada" (no data), back to Variedades. On a computer the next variety by name is selected, else the previous one, else none.
- *Recovery*: a stale `variety.has_history` closes the dialog and rereads the page, which then shows "Archivar variedad". A gone answer shows the gone notice. **Completes.**
- *Observation*: the button uses `boton--suave`, the same as Archivar, not the destructive style consolidation asked for.

**Job 2: archive a variety that has history**
- "Archivar variedad" → confirmation. It says "Te quedan 12 g. Si la archivás, dejan de verse en Inicio y en las listas, pero el stock queda guardado." when stock is above zero, a shortfall line when it is below, and the bare sentence at zero.
- Then "Sí, archivala", the toast "Variedad archivada", and the same reselection as delete. **Completes.**
- The variety then leaves the list and the pickers (EntradaStock keeps an entry's own archived variety by name).

**Job 3: find archived varieties and restore one**
- *"Archivadas (N)" at the foot of Variedades*: **not built**. Nothing in `web/src` renders it.
- *Archivadas card `/varieties/archived`*: **not built**. There is no route and it is not in `SHARED_FRONT_PATHS`.
- *Archived page, read-only, "‹ Archivadas", "Restaurar"*: **not built**. `restoreVariety` exists in `api/varieties.ts`, but no screen calls it. Opening an archived variety by URL gives the live page with no removal action, apparently with live controls the domain refuses.
- *Reserved-name refusal*: it shows inline ("Ese nombre es de una variedad archivada. Buscala en Archivadas.") and points at a screen that does not exist.
- **This job cannot be completed.** Once archived, a variety cannot be found or restored without curl.

**Job 4: a form left open while a partner archives**
- Nueva venta and EntradaStock reuse the existing gone handling (`variety.archived` is kind `gone` in `contract.json`). **Completes.**

**Job 5: existing records of an archived variety**
- No visual change. An entry edit keeps its own archived variety by name. **Completes.**

**Unchanged jobs**
- Nueva venta, Entrada de stock, price, catalog and Inicio: only fewer rows.

### Verdict

**returned**: archiving works, but the find-and-restore job has no Archivadas link, no card and no archived page, so it cannot be completed at all. The reserved-name refusal sends the seller to a screen that does not exist.
