### Comparisons

I ran `scripts/design/compare.sh` for Variedades, VariedadesCompu and VariedadDetalle. All three ran fine. The script can't compare the Archivadas card or the archived page: neither exists in `pantallas/` or in the built front.

**Variedades (phone)**
- **Fidelity:** it reads as the same design: the summary line, "Mandar catálogo", "+ Entrada de stock", "Nueva variedad", the rows, "Todas las entradas" and "+ Nueva venta". The status shows as a mark plus a word rather than a chip, which is older than this task.
- **Missing:** "Archivadas (N)" is not at the foot. **Blocking.**

**VariedadesCompu**
- **Fidelity:** the same two-pane design. Golden Cookies is selected, and the right pane is the live page.
- **Existing small differences:** the price wraps onto its own line, and the sidebar has no "+ Nueva venta". Both are older than this task.
- **Removal action:** "Archivar variedad" sits at the foot of the right pane, as specified.
- **Missing:** "Archivadas (N)", as on the phone. **Blocking.**

**VariedadDetalle (live)**
- **Fidelity:** it matches the design in structure, hierarchy and type, with the agreed "Editar" links and a read-only attribute summary.
- **Removal action:** "Archivar variedad" is a full-width suave button right after "En el catálogo". That matches the consolidated design and keeps it apart from the per-price "Borrar".
- **Not seen in an image:** the delete state, both dialogs and the stale-page refresh. The sample has history, so I rely on D1's tests for these.

**Archivadas card (`/varieties/archived`)**
- **Not built.** The route, the screen, the rows, "Archivada", "Restaurar" and the computer two-pane view are all missing.
- Commit 59ad57b records d2-archivadas as blocked because `pantallas/` holds no Archivadas screen. **Blocking.**

**Archived variety's page**
- **Not built.** There is no read-only mode, no "‹ Archivadas" and no "Restaurar". **Blocking.**

**Restore**
- No "Restaurar" and no "Variedad restaurada" toast anywhere in the front. The backend's restore route has nothing in the UI that calls it. **Blocking.**

**Root cause:** the prototype was never extended with the two new screens, though the PRD requires them in `pantallas/`. d2 needs those screens drawn, or a human decision to build them from the components named in the consolidated PRD.

### Verdict

**returned**: the Archivadas card, the archived page, "Archivadas (N)" and Restore are required by the PRD and never shipped, so archived varieties can't be found or restored from the app.

- **Observation:** no design screen exists for this card, and the comparison only shows the empty state.

**Archived variety's page**
- **Built:** "‹ Archivadas", "Archivada" in the summary line, and one suave "Restaurar" in place of "+ Entrada / Nuevo precio".
- **Removed:** "+ Entrada", "Nuevo precio", the rename, threshold and catalog forms, and the removal action. The price history is read-only (a7ccee8).
- **Observation:** the threshold and catalog sections are left out entirely rather than shown read-only.
- **Observation:** "Oculta" is not shown next to "Archivada" for a hidden archived variety.
- **Observation:** there is no screens.json entry and no drawing, so no image comparison was possible.

**Restore**
- "Restaurar" is wired on the card and on the page, with the toast "Variedad restaurada", and both lists refresh.
- The no-curl e2e covers it on phone and computer.

None of the four blocking findings from the previous round remains.

### Verdict

**approved**: every screen and state the PRD requires has now shipped and reads as the same design. The differences left are observations.

## PM: final validation (v1)

### Verdict

**returned**: UX Lead and UI Lead both found the same blocking gap.

- Delete and archive work.
- The find-and-restore job can't be done from the app: there is no "Archivadas (N)" link, no Archivadas card, no read-only archived page and no Restaurar.
- The reserved-name refusal sends the seller to Archivadas, which doesn't exist.

D2 should be built from the existing components the consolidated PRD names, without waiting for drawings in `pantallas/`. The consolidation already said these screens are built from existing components.

### For Tech Lead

1. The Variedades list (phone and computer) renders "Archivadas (N)" next to "Todas las entradas", with N taken from `GET /varieties?archived=true`, and renders nothing when N is 0.
2. `web/src/routes.ts` registers `/varieties/archived`, and the same diff adds it to `SHARED_FRONT_PATHS` in `app/static_spa.py`, to `sharedFrontRoutes` in `contract.json` and to `scripts/design/screens.json`.
3. The `/varieties/archived` screen is a card with "‹ Variedades" and no "+ Nueva venta".
   - It lists only archived varieties by name.
   - Each row has the word "Archivada" and a "Restaurar" button.
   - It shows a fixed empty line when there are none.
4. On a computer, `/varieties/archived` uses two panes and selects the first archived variety by name when nothing is selected.
5. `VariedadDetalle` has a read-only mode when the variety's `archived` is true.
   - Its back arrow is "‹ Archivadas"; with no history, back goes to Archivadas.
   - It shows "Archivada" in words.
   - It renders none of "+ Entrada", "Nuevo precio", price delete, the threshold switch, rename, any "Editar" or the removal action.
   - "Restaurar" is its only action.
6. At least one screen calls `restoreVariety`, in one tap with no confirmation. On success it shows the toast "Variedad restaurada", with no name or amount, and refreshes the live and archived lists.
7. Tests:
   - A front test covers the count and its absence, the card listing only archived varieties, restore from the card and from the page, and the archived page offering only Restaurar.
   - A `web/e2e` run deletes a fresh variety, archives one with a sale, finds it in Archivadas, opens its read-only page and restores it.

## PM: final validation

### Verdict

**approved**: UX Lead's final walk and UI Lead's final comparisons both approve, and neither reports a blocking finding. Every job in the validated flow can be completed: delete, archive, find and restore, a form left open while a partner archives, and existing records of an archived variety. None of the seven claims returned last round is still open. The Archivadas link, the card, the read-only archived page and Restaurar are built, and the no-curl e2e covers them.

Both leads checked by reading the code and running the comparisons. No running app was reachable, so nobody clicked through it live, and the sample app has no archived variety to show in the images. These points, and the friction below, go to the PR as observations, not blockers:

- "Eliminar variedad" uses the suave button style, not the destructive one.
- After restoring from the archived page, the page turns into the live variety in place instead of going back to Archivadas. Nothing is lost either way.
- The archived page leaves out "Oculta" for a hidden archived variety. The flag itself is kept and comes back on restore.
- The archived page leaves out the threshold and catalog sections instead of showing them read-only.
- "Archivada" uses the "Oculta" text style, not a neutral Insignia, and the live status mark still shows next to it. The state is still told in words.
- No drawing or `screens.json` entry exists for the archived page, so it could not be compared against an image.

### Integration

All seven subtask branches were already in `agentic/GH-117` when I started (a1, a2, c, b, e, d1, d2); `git merge-base --is-ancestor` confirmed each one. There was nothing to merge and no conflicts.

Checks on the merged branch, first full run: `pytest`, `ruff`, `mypy`, web typecheck, web lint and web tests all passed. `web-e2e` had 33 passed and 1 failed, `catalog.spec.ts:54` ("Entrada guardada" not visible). The merge itself broke nothing.

The open gap was the one the findings list: **d2-archivadas was never delivered**, because its entry condition was unmet (no drawings in `pantallas/`). The PM's final validation said to build it from existing components, so I did. This closes the seven blocking `PM: final validation` rows and the d2 plan-scope rows.

- **Routes:**
  - `ARCHIVED_VARIETIES = '/varieties/archived'` is in `web/src/routes.ts` and registered in `router.tsx` (`chrome: 'card'`).
  - It is also added to `SHARED_FRONT_PATHS` (`app/static_spa.py`), `contract.json`'s `sharedFrontRoutes` and `scripts/design/screens.json`.
  - The existing contract and route checks pass.
  - Invariant 6 does not apply: no refusal code is involved.
- **"Archivadas (N)"** sits at the foot of `Variedades.tsx`, after "Todas las entradas". N comes from `GET /varieties?archived=true`, and the link is absent when N is 0.
- **Archivadas card** (`ArchivedVarieties.tsx`, `ArchivedIndex.tsx`):
  - It has "‹ Variedades" and no "+ Nueva venta".
  - Rows are sorted by name, each with the word "Archivada" and a "Restaurar" button. A fixed line shows when there are none.
  - On a computer, `VarietiesLayout` shows the card in the left pane, both on that route and beside an archived variety's page.
  - With nothing selected, it selects the first archived variety by name.
- **Read-only page:** `VarietyPage` switches on `archived`.
  - The back arrow is "‹ Archivadas" and "Archivada" shows in the summary line.
  - Stock comes from `GET /stock?archived=true` (new `readArchivedStock` and `keys.archivedStock`).
  - No "+ Entrada", "Nuevo precio", price delete (new `PriceRows` `readOnly`), threshold section, "Editar", forms or removal action.
  - "Restaurar" is its only action.
- **Restore** (`useRestore.tsx`): one tap, no confirmation, toast "Variedad restaurada" with no data. It invalidates varieties and stock, and stock also reaches the catalog. A gone answer shows the generic gone notice.
- **Tests:**
  - `Archivadas.test.tsx` has 8 tests: the count and its absence, the card listing only archived varieties, restore from the card, a gone restore, the page's read-only contents, and restore from the page.
  - `web/e2e/archivadas.spec.ts` deletes a fresh variety, archives one with a stock entry, finds it in Archivadas, opens its read-only page, restores it, and asserts zero console messages.
  - `flashReducer.test.ts` has two new tests for the flash fix below.

**Two real bugs the e2e found:**
1. d1's `VarietyRemoval.leave()` invalidated `varieties` with an active refetch. The deleted variety's page was still mounted and read it back, giving two 404s in the browser console. It now passes `{ refetchType: 'none' }`.
2. This was the cause of the `catalog.spec.ts` flake (it had failed in both the first run and the delivery presubmit, so it was not noise).
   - On a computer, `VarietiesIndex` replaces `/varieties` with the first variety once the list loads.
   - A toast shown after leaving a form was dismissed by that location change whenever the list loaded after the toast appeared.
   - I added `flash.keepAcrossReplace()` and a `ReplaceTo` component. `VarietiesIndex` and `ArchivedIndex` now use it, so the toast survives the one replacement. The next location change still dismisses it.

After the changes I ran only the touched tests, not the full suite again. That was 972 web tests, `tsc`, `oxlint`, `ruff` and `mypy`, plus the `archivadas` and `catalog` e2e specs, which passed twice in a row.

I left the other `Tech Lead: findings` rows (the empty panel `today` and `week` periods in the books-unchanged capture, `tests/fakes/archive.py` writing the row directly, the `ReferenceRefused` observations) untouched. They concern code that is already merged and green.

### For the Tech Lead

Look first at `VarietyPage.tsx` (the `archived` branches), `VarietiesLayout.tsx` (which pane is the card), `useRestore.tsx`, and the flash change (`flashReducer.ts`, `ReplaceTo.tsx`).

- **No design drawings.** The card and the archived page are built from the list's existing rows and buttons. `screens.json` has an `Archivadas` entry that reuses the `Variedades` design, so its comparison will show a mismatch until design draws it. There is no `VariedadArchivada` entry, because `scripts/design/sample_app.py` has no archived variety to point `{low}` at. I did not run `compare.sh`, so no design comparisons are attached.
- **Not done:**
  - The price history page (`/varieties/{id}/prices`) still shows "Borrar" for an archived variety. The API refuses it as `variety.archived`; only the variety page's own price section is read-only.
  - The archived page also fetches the live `GET /stock`. The result is unused, so it is one wasted request.
- **e2e flakiness:**
  - The new spec failed once in a parallel run at the Archivadas card link click. I did not find the cause.
  - I changed it to wait for the auto-selected `/varieties/{id}` URL before checking the page. It then passed three times, including twice next to `catalog.spec.ts`.
  - In that spec the "Restaurar" click uses `.last()`, because two panes each have the button.

**This round**, answering `Tech Lead: review integration` and the open subtask rows:
- **Blocking, no computer-mode test.** `Archivadas.test.tsx` now has a "on a computer (two panes)" block over the real `VarietiesLayout`, `ArchivedIndex` and `VarietiesIndex`. All of these are in `web/src/screens/varieties/Archivadas.test.tsx`; the file has 11 tests in total.

  - It checks that the left pane is the Archivadas card beside an archived page and returns to the live list once the variety is restored.
- **Price history read-only.** `PriceHistory` passes `readOnly={variety.archived}`, so no "Borrar" shows on an archived variety. A new test in `PriceHistory.test.tsx` covers it.

  - The phone path drives the card, the "‹ Archivadas" back link and Restaurar on the page.

- **Token.** `.variedad-archivada__restaurar` uses `height: var(--alto-tocable)`. I dropped the hard-coded font-size, since there is no font-size token.
- **Observations.** After an archive, `VarietyRemoval` now actively refetches the archived list so a mounted "Archivadas (N)" is not stale. `VarietyCatalogSection` is guarded with `!archived`.
- **Books-unchanged seed.** `tests/varieties/test_archive_moves_no_figure.py` now has a sale on the clock's own day (2026-10-15). The test asserts `sales_count >= 1` in the panel's `today` and `week` periods before comparing the three captures, so a filter there would be caught.
- **`tests/fakes/archive.py`.** `archive_by_hand` now archives through `Variety.archive` and the real `SqlVarieties`, no longer by writing the column. I removed the local copy in `test_varieties_api.py` in favour of the shared helper.

### Integration

All seven subtask branches (a1, a2, c, b, e, d1, d2) were already merged into `agentic/GH-117` before this run. Nothing was out and no conflicts came up. This round only fixes what the Tech Lead's review of `9bc4798` returned:

- **`web/e2e/archivadas.spec.ts` (blocking, flaky phone run).** After opening the archived page, the spec expected a link named "Rosa". A phone's archived page has only the heading "Rosa", so the step passed only while the Archivadas card was still unmounting. It now asserts `getByRole('heading', { name: 'Rosa' })` on both viewports. `npm --prefix web run e2e -- e2e/archivadas.spec.ts --repeat-each=5` passed all 10 runs (5 phone, 5 computer).
- **`tests/stock/test_read_stock_api.py` (important).** `test_an_archived_variety_leaves_the_rows_but_its_grams_stay_in_the_total` wrote `row.archived_at = NOW` on the row directly. It now calls the shared `archive_by_hand` helper, which goes through `Variety.archive` and `SqlVarieties.save`. The `Session` and `VarietyRow` imports it no longer needs are gone. `pytest tests/stock/test_read_stock_api.py` passes. `ruff check .`, `mypy app tests`, `npm --prefix web run typecheck` and `npm --prefix web run lint` pass.

Earlier in this task the toast flake in `catalog.spec.ts` was fixed at its root, with `keepAcrossReplace` and `ReplaceTo`. The delete 404 console messages were fixed with `refetchType: 'none'`. The computer-mode Archivadas tests, the read-only price history, the books-unchanged seed and the shared archive helper were also settled in earlier rounds.

### For the Tech Lead

- **Look first:** the one-line assertion change at `web/e2e/archivadas.spec.ts:83-84`, and the call to `archive_by_hand` in `tests/stock/test_read_stock_api.py`. No production code, route, refusal code or schema changed this round, so invariants 1-8 are not touched.

  - The design drawings and comparisons for Archivadas and VariedadArchivada under `docs/product/design-system/pantallas/` do not exist. The screens were built from existing components and tokens. `scripts/design/screens.json` has an Archivadas entry but no VariedadArchivada entry, because the sample app has no archived variety.
  - The backend observation rows are untouched. `SqlVarieties.save` raises `ReferenceRefused` for a row that is gone, but only `record_price_entry` catches it, so a rename, archive, restore or price delete racing a delete still answers 500. A concurrent delete that finds no row makes `delete_variety` answer 409 `variety.has_history` instead of a gone code. `flush_or_refuse` maps every `IntegrityError` to `ReferenceRefused`, so a non-reference failure is mislabelled, though it stays a 500 as invariant 5 requires.
